**Bu hujjat 3 qism:** (0) Sintez-xulosa - (1) TO'LIQ 99-agent tahlil (20 soha x 5 nuqtai-nazar) - (2) Master reja fazalar.

# 0-QISM: SINTEZ XULOSA

Verified: legacy `orders` table no longer exists (that two-world is resolved), but the 1-card=1-seat atomic invariant IS violated (multiple cards hold 2 employees), and CRM deal→sales_order link is 0/4 (golden thread head is broken). I have enough live evidence. Writing the report.

# EUROPRINT ERP — VIZYON-MOSLIK HALOL HISOBOTI

> O'lchov = EGASI VIZYONI (Q-40), "ishlaydi" emas. Har asosiy da'vo JONLI tasdiqlandi (kod fayl:satr + DB `_audit/q.cjs`). Sana: 2026-06-25.

---

## 1. UMUMIY VIZYON-MOSLIK: ~45%

**Egasi "hammasi vizyonga to'g'ri kelmaydi" deydi — bu QISMAN to'g'ri, lekin "hammasi axlat" emas.** Halol holat: **skelet/struktura ko'p joyda VIZYONGA YAQIN qurilgan (jadvallar, endpointlar, FE sahifalar mavjud), lekin VIZYON YURAGI — karta-markazlilik, oltin-ip oqimi va kunlik gate-lar — JONLI ULANMAGAN yoki BO'SH.** Ya'ni "ballonsiz mashina" metaforasi hozir ham to'g'ri: dvigatel (modullar) bor, transmissiya (ulanish + data) yo'q. 2 ta soha haqiqatan vizyonga yaqin (Moliya/GL mexanikasi, Razryad strukturasi), qolgan 18 sohada model to'g'ri lekin oqim/data uzuq.

**Eng og'ir haqiqat:** 4 ta MARKAZIY vizyon-printsipining hammasi buzilgan — (1) karta-markazli login/oylik, (2) oltin-ip uzluksiz oqim, (3) ЦКП kunlik gate, (4) bitta yagona daraxt. Bular vizyonning "miyasi" — ularsiz qolgan hamma narsa eski model ustida ishlaydi.

---

## 3. ENG KATTA VIZYON-CHETLASHISHLARI (P0)

### P0-1 — Karta-markazli login + oylik UMUMAN YO'Q (vizyonning #1 printsipi)
- **Vizyon:** `card_id` NULL → login YO'Q + oylik YO'Q. Oylik kartaning salary maydonlaridan + razryad-koeffitsientdan keladi.
- **Build:** `users.card_id` ustuni **DB'da umuman yo'q** (JONLI: information_schema → faqat `position_id`, `role`). Oylik `payroll.service.ts:132` da `Number(raw['baseSalary'] ?? raw['base_salary'] ?? 0)` — kartaning salary maydoni ISHLATILMAYDI. `razryad_levels.salary_max`=NULL bo'lgani uchun kartadan hisoblash baribir mumkin emas.
- **Fakt:** `apps/api/src/modules/hr/payroll/payroll.service.ts:132`; DB users-da card_id yo'q; login-gate `login.service.ts:126` env-flag `CARD_LOGIN_GATE_ENABLED='true'` talab qiladi (default OFF).

### P0-2 — Golden-thread eventlari hech qachon yozilmagan (oltin ip uzuq)
- **Vizyon:** SD→PP→MES→QC→WMS→FIN uzluksiz, outbox pattern + at-least-once delivery, 19 domain event.
- **Build:** `domain_events`=**0 qator** (JONLI). Eventlar faqat in-process EventEmitter2 orqali; restart/crash → yo'qoladi. CRM deal→sales_order link = **0/4** (JONLI) — zanjirning BOSHI ham uzilgan.
- **Fakt:** DB `SELECT count(*) FROM domain_events` = 0; `crm_deals` linked=0/total=4.

### P0-3 — ЦКП kunlik gate va data BUTUNLAY BO'SH
- **Vizyon:** har karta kunlik ЦКП o'lchov; mashinasiz xodimlar AI-chatbot orqali hisobot; deadline o'tmasa kun-oyligi yo'q.
- **Build:** `ckp_fact_values`=**0 qator** (JONLI). AI-chatbot kunlik savol YO'Q, IoT/MES→ЦКП feed YO'Q, payroll ЦКП-gate so'ramaydi.
- **Fakt:** DB ckp_fact_values=0; jadval mavjud lekin oziqlanmaydi.

### P0-4 — Bitta yagona daraxt INVARIANTI buzilgan + 1-karta=1-seat buzilgan
- **Vizyon:** 1 Egasi-ildiz → bitta daraxt; 1 karta = 1 o'rindiq = 1 xodim (atomik).
- **Build:** `org_departments`'da **19 root** (parent_id NULL) — bitta daraxt emas (JONLI). `employee_org_departments`'da bir nechta karta 2 xodim ko'taradi (JONLI: dept 34/32/24/19/29 = 2 xodim).
- **Fakt:** DB roots=19/total=144; employee_org_departments GROUP BY → bir nechta karta c=2.

### P0-5 — RBAC eski positions modelida (karta-markazli emas)
- **Vizyon:** RBAC kartadan (ko'rish/qilish/tasdiq), karta o'zgarsa ruxsat o'zgaradi.
- **Build:** PermissionGuard `position_permissions` jadvalini `users.position_id`'dan tekshiradi; org_departments rbac_tier data 0/144. Eski parallel model davom etadi.
- **Fakt:** users-da position_id mavjud (card_id emas); rbac-tier.policy.ts manba lekin karta-data bo'sh.

### P0-6 — Razryad + head_user_id egasi-data BO'SH
- **Vizyon:** razryad har kartada, dinamik o'sish zanjiri; xodim-karta link.
- **Build:** 0/144 kartada `razryad_level_id` (JONLI); `head_user_id` 18/144 (126 NULL). razryad_levels seed bor lekin exam_pass_threshold/max_retakes/salary_min/max = barchasi NULL.
- **Fakt:** DB with_razryad=0/144; with_head=18/144; razryad_levels row inspeksiyasi → threshold NULL.

---

## 4. VIZYONGA MOS QISMLAR (halol)

- **Moliya / GL mexanikasi (~70%, eng yetuk):** atomik journal (`insertJournal` + db.transaction), FIFO/FEFO, kanonik `entries` (6 qator JONLI). Bitta GL modeli (vizyonga mos). *Lekin:* cashier_movements=0, kassir UI konsepti noto'g'ri.
- **Razryad strukturasi (~57%):** 6-level seed, coefficient (1.00/1.25/...), 2-imzo tasdiq workflow blueprint, FE RazryadTab badge. Struktura vizyonga sodiq — faqat egasi-data va karta-biriktirish yetishmaydi.
- **Eski "ikki dunyo" qisman hal bo'lgan:** legacy `orders` jadvali **endi DB'da yo'q** (JONLI: "отношение orders не существует") — `sales_orders` kanonik bo'ldi. Bu eski memory'dagi parallel-order muammosi yopilgan.
- **Aisha Layer A skeleti** mavjud (~30 tool, web miya), futuristik UI — vizyon yo'nalishida (lekin Layer B yo'q, tool-loop test qilinmagan).

---

## 5. NEGA SHUNDAY BO'LGAN (ildiz-sabab)

Asosiy sabab — **vizyon kechroq, build erta**: tizimning katta qismi karta-markazli model TO'LIQ tasdiqlanmasidan oldin (eski "positions/employees-markazli" model bo'yicha) qurilgan, keyin karta-vizyon (2026-06 intervyu) ustiga "qoplama" sifatida qo'shilgan. Natijada DDL/jadvallar yangi vizyonga moslab QO'SHILGAN (org_departments, razryad_levels, ckp_*, cashier_movements, domain_events), lekin (a) ESKI yozish-yo'llari hali ham eski jadvallarga yozadi (payroll→baseSalary, RBAC→position_id), (b) yangi jadvallar OZIQLANTIRILMAGAN (data=0), (c) egasi bermagan MASTER-DATA bo'sh qoldirilgan (head_user_id, razryad qiymatlari, salary band, ЦКП norma — "fabrikatsiya taqiq" qoidasi bo'yicha to'g'ri bo'sh qoldirilgan). Gate-lar (login, oylik, darslik, ЦКП) env-flag bilan OFF yoki ulanmagan — chunki data bo'sh holatda yoqilsa butun tizim bloklanardi. Ya'ni: **struktura ~vizyon, lekin transmissiya ulanmagan va idishlar bo'sh.**

---

## 6. NIMA QILISH KERAK (vizyonga yetkazish yo'li)

**Avval EGASI-QARORI (data — muhandislik kuta olmaydi):**
1. `org_departments`'ni **bitta daraxtga** keltirish: 19 root → 1 Egasi-ildiz + 7 otdeleniye (egasi kim-kimni-boshqaradi sxemasini berishi). `head_user_id` 126 NULL'ni to'ldirish.
2. Razryad qiymatlari: salary band (dan-gacha), exam_pass_threshold, min_months (vizyon ≥3) — har razryad uchun.
3. ЦКП norma + deadline (spec 16 soat ╳ 3 soat ZIDDIYATINI hal qilish), kurs↔karta biriktirish.
4. RBAC tier (har karta), AI-kalit.

**Keyin MUHANDISLIK (ustuvorlik tartibi — markaziy printsiplardan):**
1. **Karta-markazli yadro (P0-1, P0-4, P0-5):** `users.card_id` ustunini qo'shish; payroll'ni karta salary+razryad-koeffga ko'chirish; RBAC'ni position_id'dan card'ga; 1-seat unique guard. Bu vizyonning "miyasi" — birinchi.
2. **Golden-thread oqimi (P0-2):** outbox pattern (domain_events'ga atomik yozish) + relay; CRM deal→SO avto-yaratish; 12 orphan SD→PP ulash. Real DB-proof bilan.
3. **ЦКП + gate-lar (P0-3):** MES/IoT→ЦКП feed listener; AI kunlik chatbot; oylik-gate (ЦКП+darslik) ulash. Data kelgach yoqish.
4. **Login-gate yoqish (P0-1):** karta-data to'lgach `CARD_LOGIN_GATE_ENABLED=true`.
5. Director 5-ko'rsatkich holat-formula hisobi, IoT operator-login, Frontend EP-token tozalash — keyingi to'lqin.

**Eng muhim strategik tavsiya:** har modulni alohida "to'liq qilish" o'rniga, AVVAL bitta vertikal ip'ni (1 karta → login → oylik → ЦКП → 1 buyurtma SD→PP→MES→QC→WMS→FIN→GL) UCHMA-UCH JONLI ishlatib ko'rsatish. Hozir har modul 40-50% lekin hech qaysi UCHMA-UCH zanjir jonli emas — bitta to'liq ip ko'rsatilsa, qolgani shablon bo'ladi.

# 1-QISM: TO'LIQ 99-AGENT TAHLIL

_Har soha 5 nuqtai-nazardan: vizyon nimani kutadi / nima qurilgan / chetlashishlar / jonli isbot._

## 1. CKP / maqsad / KPI - o'rtacha 29% (6 tahlil)

### Asosiy g'oya / Model - `PARTIAL` 18%
- **Vizyon kutadi:** ЦКП (Goal Setting & Delivery) is the KPI framework central to the card-centric org model (FAZA-05, EP-ORG-014..018 in decisions/01-org-kartalar.md). Vision expects: (1) **Master definition by HR:** each card has a ЦКП text + measurement unit (SON/FOIZ/VAQT) + target norm (tskp_target). (2) **Auto-feed for operators:** IoT/MES automatically records daily facts (ckp_fact_values table). (3) **AI chatbot for non-machine roles:** AI asks daily KPI question; responses trigger kunlik-hisobot (ckp_chat_logs). (4) **Cascading aggregation:** card facts roll up to department→otdeleniye; formula applies (SON quantity%/FOIZ percent/VAQT duration/boolean yes-no). (5) **Daily deadline gate:** 16 hours or 3 hours (per decision OCHIQ-JAVOBLAR) to submit; failure = no oylik that day (payroll gate). (6) **Fact table (ckp_fact_values):** card_id, employee_id, product_id, fact_date, actual_value, target_value, achievement_pct, formula_type, status, source (MANUAL/AI_CHAT/MES_AUTO/IOT), submitted_at, recorded_by, notes. (7) **Error catalog per card:** xato-katalog for quick issue categorization. (8) **Multi-product slots:** mashinasiz xodim may track multiple products/deliverables per day. (9) **AI autofill:** @FAZA-10 markaziy AI crosses ЦКП sources (MES/QC/davomat) with portret/requirements → grade+baho+past-moslik warn (don't block). Vision = ЦКП drives accountability; automation frees humans to focus on exceptions.
- **Qurilgan:** **Structure: PARTIAL.** (1) ckp_fact_values table EXISTS with correct schema: card_id, employee_id, product_id, fact_date, target_value, actual_value, achievement_pct, source enum (MANUAL/AI_CHAT/MES_AUTO/IOT), formula_type, status, submitted_at, recorded_by, notes (column_count = 14, correct). (2) org_departments has tskp (description text, 22/139 filled), tskp_target (INTEGER, 0/139 filled), tskp_measurement_unit (VARCHAR enum SON/FOIZ/VAQT, 0/139 filled), ckp_formula_type (NULL 0/139), ckp_frequency (NULL 0/139), ckp_report_deadline_hours (NULL 0/139). (3) **CkpFactRepository & CkpFactService BUILT:** upsertFact(), listByCard(), aggregateByDate() with cascading SUM+AVG logic, Result<T> pattern, parametrized SQL. (4) **CkpController HTTP routes LIVE:** POST /org-structure/ckp/fact (Zod-validated FactSchema), GET /org-structure/ckp/fact (list by cardId+date range), GET /org-structure/ckp/aggregate/:cardId (cascade). (5) **Achievement formula: PARTIAL CODED** (ckp-fact.service.ts:29-34) — boolean→100/0, quantity_pct→(actual/target)×100, default=0 if target=null (fabrication guard: no fake data). (6) **DB-PROOF: 0 rows in ckp_fact_values** (no daily facts recorded yet); 25/144 cards have tskp text, but formula_type/frequency/deadline_hours all NULL (owner-data not yet filled). (7) **Missing:** ai_ckp_chat_logs table exists but has 0 rows; AI daily question feature NOT WIRED (no endpoint to POST AI-generated daily question, no recurring AI-query cron). (8) **Missing:** IoT/MES auto-feed NOT CONNECTED (MES module doesn't emit/listen to ckp_fact_values INSERT; IoT tablet doesn't know about ckp_fact_values.source='IOT'). (9) **Missing:** payroll kunlik-gate NOT HOOKED (calculate-payroll.handler never checks ckp_fact_values deadline or presence for oylik deduction). (10) **Missing:** error-catalog table, xato-katalog seed, error-quick-pick UI. (11) **Missing:** multi-product slot handling (UI for adding multiple rows per card per day). (12) **Code quality:** CkpFactRepository uses typed SQL with parametrized queries (safe); CkpFactService applies formula correctly; controller validates DTO + guards applied. No green-lie: actually calls .upsertFact() → real DB INSERT. FABRICATION GUARD: if tskp_target=NULL, achievement=0 (doesn't fake 100%, correctly flags as "no norm, can't measure").
- **Chetlashishlar:**
  - **[P0]** payroll kunlik-gate MISSING: calculate-payroll.handler never queries ckp_fact_values or checks deadline; oylik calculated without ЦКП-gate - _vizyon:_ If ckp_fact_values has no 'submitted' record by 16-hour deadline (or 3 hours per spec), that day's oylik = 0 (gate blocks payment). Payroll must check ckp_report_deadline_hours and fact_date, enforce deadline, sum oylik only for days with valid facts. _/ build:_ calculate-payroll.handler (finance/payroll.service.ts:130-180) calculates oylik from org_departments salary_type/min/max and baseSalary, iterates employees—NO card_id CHECK, NO ckp_fact_values query, NO deadline-gate. LmsCompletionService (darslik gate) is 'DO NOT touch' stub (line 154).
  - **[P0]** AI daily ЦКП question MISSING: ai_ckp_chat_logs table exists but 0 rows; no recurring AI-query cron; no chatbot integration to ask 'Bugungi ЦКП?' - _vizyon:_ @FAZA-10 markaziy AI should query each mashinasiz (non-machine) xodim daily via Telegram/web chat: 'What was your ЦКП today?' (SON units/FOIZ %/VAQT hours) + parse response → ckp_fact_values INSERT with source='AI_CHAT'. Batch or per-card scheduled task. _/ build:_ ai_ckp_chat_logs jadval mavjud (DB), but zero rows + zero writes. AiFitService (ai/ai.service.ts:67) sends prompt + writes ai_fit_scores (1 row live), but NO ckp-scoring logic, NO 'daily question' workflow. No endpoint POST /ckp/ai-ask or cron @Cron('0 16 * * *') to fire daily.
  - **[P0]** IoT/MES auto-feed NOT WIRED: MES module doesn't emit/listen to ckp_fact_values; operator tablet doesn't write 'today achieved 150 units' - _vizyon:_ MES production session (smena) end-event or IoT production-complete event should trigger ckp_fact_values UPSERT: card_id+employee_id+fact_date+actual_value (machine-counted units/kg/m²) + source='MES_AUTO' or 'IOT'. Automatic, no manual recording for machine roles. _/ build:_ MES module (mes/production-sessions.controller.ts) tracks production but doesn't emit ProductionCompleted→CkpFactCreated event. IoT module (iot/*) collects sensor data but no route to ckp_fact_values. CkpFactService.recordFact() accepts source='MES_AUTO'/'IOT' enum but nothing writes to it (only manual @Post /fact likely). Listener registry in org-structure.module.ts lists DepartmentCreatedEvent, not production-events.
  - **[P0]** ЦКП target/formula/frequency owner-data NOT FILLED: tskp_target 0/139, ckp_formula_type 0/139, ckp_frequency 0/139, ckp_report_deadline_hours 0/139 - _vizyon:_ Each card must have egasi-populated: (1) tskp_target (numeric norm, e.g., 100 units/day), (2) ckp_formula_type (enum: 'quantity_pct'/'foiz'/'vaqt'/'boolean'), (3) ckp_frequency (daily/weekly/monthly enum), (4) ckp_report_deadline_hours (16 or 3, per spec ziddiyat). Without these, achievement% = 0 always (formula guard) → payment blocked. Fabrikatsiya taqiq—must come from egasi or HR, not hardcoded. _/ build:_ Table columns exist; no defaults (correct, prevents fabrication). Service.calcAchievement() defaults formula_type='quantity_pct' if NULL; target=NULL→achievement=0. UI (OrgNodeDetail card-fields edit) may accept these inputs, but no form validation that they're required before card becomes 'active'. No API endpoint restricts card-activation until deadline_hours is set. Seed: 0 rows populated.
  - **[P1]** Multi-product slot UI MISSING: cannot add 2+ deliverables per card per day (mashinasiz xodim tracking 3 products simultaneously) - _vizyon:_ Card detail should show: [+Add Product] button → inline form (product_id+actual_value) → each row = separate ckp_fact_values INSERT with same card_id+employee_id+fact_date, different product_id. Error-catalog per row (defect category: missed-deadline/quality/waste/rework). _/ build:_ CkpFactInput interface accepts product_id (optional), table has product_id FK (nullable). Controller FactSchema.parse allows 1 (product_id?: z.number().optional()). UI/form for adding multiple products per day = 0 lines found. productId likely points to materials/products table, but no multi-row form in card UI.
  - **[P1]** Error-catalog table MISSING: no xato-katalog per card for quick issue categorization (e.g., defect types: 'missed-deadline', 'quality', 'material-waste', 'rework') - _vizyon:_ Each card should have associated error-catalog (errors or defect-reasons): 'Nima xato/sabab bo'ldi?' dropdown on daily-report form. ckp_fact_values.error_reason FK → error_catalog. Admin-seed 10-15 generic errors; card-specific errors added via card detail. _/ build:_ No xato_katalog or error_reason FK in ckp_fact_values. ckp-fact.service notes (text 2000 char) for free-form note; no structured defect-dropdown. Error-catalog table = 0 (grep 'xato_katalog'/'defect_reason'/'error_type' = 0 matches in schema).
  - **[P2]** Cascading aggregation UI MISSING: no card-hierarchy dashboard showing 'Card → Department → Otdeleniye' KPI rollup (avg achievement% by level) - _vizyon:_ Card detail's 'ЦКП' tab should show: (1) daily facts list (latest 14 days), (2) cascade: 'Your avg 72% | Your dept avg 81% | Otdeleniye avg 65%' + trend graph. Tsifralar come from aggregateByDate() CTE in repo. _/ build:_ aggregateByDate(cardId, date) CTE queries WITH RECURSIVE subtree + AVG(achievement_pct), returns {fact_count, avg_achievement}. No UI consumes this endpoint. CkpController GET /aggregate/:cardId (live, 200 returns JSON), but no tab or dashboard displays the response. FE /org-structure/card/:id has no 'ЦКП' tab component to render facts+cascade.
  - **[P2]** Kunlik deadline enforcement UI MISSING: no 'ЦКП status for today' badge showing 'SUBMITTED (14:32)' vs 'PENDING 2h left' vs 'OVERDUE -3h' on card-header - _vizyon:_ Card page should show red OVERDUE alert if deadline_hours passed without submitted fact (or at 1h-remaining yellow); xodim knows immediately 'need to submit by 16:00'. On dashboard 'cards needing ЦКП submission TODAY' list. _/ build:_ GET /ckp/fact returns list (may include today's record or empty if not submitted), but no real-time 'hours left' calculation in FE. No cron to mark OVERDUE or alert Telegram @16:01. Status logic faqat in-DB: submitted_at populated on upsert = record exists. Deadline_hours value = NULL = can't calculate deadline.
  - **[P2]** Formula-type UI + toggle MISSING: card-detail doesn't show 'ЦКП formula: Quantity % vs FOIZ vs VAQT vs Boolean' selector; can't switch formula on-the-fly - _vizyon:_ Card 'ЦКП formula type' dropdown (enum: 'quantity_pct'/'foiz'/'vaqt'/'boolean') + tooltip explaining each (e.g., 'Quantity %: actual/target×100'). Changing formula recalculates historical achievement%. Egasi/HR tunes per-card. _/ build:_ Table column ckp_formula_type (VARCHAR NULL) exists. No UI form field in OrgNodeDetail or card edit dialog to set/view it. Service.calcAchievement() hard-defaults 'quantity_pct' if NULL (line 42), so formula never changes—stuck on 1 formula forever unless DB direct PATCH.

### Oqim / Integratsiya - `PARTIAL` 15%
- **Vizyon kutadi:** **ЦКП (Qiymatli Yakuniy mahsulot)** — har karta uchun kunlik bajarilish KPI. VIZYON:
1. **MATN va O'LCHOV**: HR ta'rifni yozadi (markaziy); o'lchov turi = SON/FOIZ/VAQT (kar taga moslab) — EP-ORG-049, EP-ORG-111.
2. **NORMA**: kartada standart norma + xodimga shaxsiy tuzatish — EP-ORG-051.
3. **FORMULA-TURI**: 4 tur (miqdor%/sifat/muddat%/holat) — EP-ORG-130.
4. **KUNLIK HISOBOT DEADLINE**: faqat maskinasiz xodimlarga; 16 soat (yakuniy raqam egasida) — HR-H3.
5. **FAKT-QIYMAT TIZIMI**: kunlik INSERT (ckp_fact_values); idempotent; employee+product+date key; achievement% formula-turiga qarab — EP-ORG-014..018.
6. **KASKAD-AGREGAT**: quyi kartalar → yuqori kartaga avto-to'planadi (o'rtacha achievement) — EP-ORG-112.
7. **AI-CHATBOT**: mashinasiz xodim kunlik botga savol; hisobot deadline o'tmasa kun-oylik yo'q — HR-H3, Q-29.
8. **IoT/MES AVTO-FEED**: mashinali ishlar MES/IoT→karta avto-yechadi — Q-113 avto-statistika.
9. **OYLIK-GATE**: ЦКП kunlik deadline o'tmasa (yoki darslik tugamasa) kun-oylik = 0 — EP-ORG-049 (KARTALAR Q15), EP-LMS-027 (darslik-gate).
10. **MULTIZUMO**: bitta karta multi-product/employee slot bilan; har biriga alohida fakt — EP-ORG-096.

Vyzon mosliги = oqim: **CRM→SD (buyurtma)→PP (reja)→MES (ishlab chiqarish)→QC (sifat)→WMS (ombor)→FIN**. ЦКП oziqlanadi: (1) MES/IoT (mashinali norma), (2) Xodim kunlik savol (botga), (3) QC (sifat defekt), (4) Manual (HR). Yig'indi → oylik-hisobda gate (tuzatma/bonus) + AI reyting.

**Xato-katalogi** (mishalar): har karta tipik xatolar ro'yxati; hodisa shundan tanlanadi → statistika (Q-097). **AI baho IKKI TOMONLAMA**: xato − + yaxshi ish + (Q-98). **Adolat prinsipi**: AI belgilaydi lekin salbiy ta'sir (jarima/ball/razryad-pasayish) FAQAT inson tasdig'idan (Q-82, Q-93, Q-134).

### UI / UX - `DIVERGENT` 24%
- **Vizyon kutadi:** ЦКП (GSD/KPI) — kunlik o'lchanadigan karta natijasi. Vizyon qoida: (1) KARTA-MARKAZli — har karta ЦКП ta'rifi + norma + formula-turi; xodim mashinasiz bo'lsa AI-chatbot kunlik savol; mashinachi IoT/MES avto-feed. (2) FORM saqlashi HAQIQIY — kunlik fakt kiritish → BE /org-structure/ckp/fact POST → DB ckp_fact_values REAL INSERT → forma submit qaytgach qayta-refresh'da ko'rinadi. (3) Deadline GATE — 16 soat ichida hisobot bo'lmasa o'sha kun oylik gate=false (ai_ckp_scores.salary_gate_pass). (4) Kaskad-agregat — karta ЦКП → bo'lim/otdeleniye o'rtacha avtomatik. (5) Graceful fallback — AI yo'q bo'lsa template-savol. User ko'radi: A) ЦКП tab/panel (org-structure ichida yoki HR dashboard'da), B) kunlik kiritish forma (xodim uchun hozirgi-sana, ЦКП savollar), C) tarix jadval (o'tgan kunlarning fakt), D) kaskad-agregat metrika (bo'lim/otdeleniye o'rtacha); barcha forma saqlash POST bilan REAL bo'ladi, ECHO/stub yo'q. Egasi-data: ЦКП norma qiymatları, deadline soat (16 yoki 3), formula-turi per-karta — bu faza YOZILMAYDI, nur mexanizmi quriladi.
- **Qurilgan:** Qisman qurilgan. BE endpoint qurilgan (CkpController POST /org-structure/ckp/fact, list, aggregate), schema Drizzle'da (ckp_fact_values 47 ustun, ai_ckp_scores 13 ustun), repository+service Result<T> pattern bilan. ### To'g'rilik (ishlaydi != to'g'ri) - `PARTIAL` 35%
- **Vizyon kutadi:** **Vizyon (MASSIV-100 PHASE-05 + OCHIQ-JAVOBLAR):**

1. **ЦКП = Goal Setting & Daily measurement (maqsad o'rnatish + kunlik o'lchov)**
   - Har karta (org_functions): `tskp` (maqsad matn) + `tskp_target` (norma/qiymat) + `tskp_measurement_unit` (o'lchov birligi) + `ckp_formula_type` (formula: boolean/quantity_pct/quality/duration)
   - `ckp_frequency` (kunlik/haftalik/oylik) + `ckp_report_deadline_hours` (hisobot muddati, masalan 16 soat)
   - Mashinasiz xodimlar bot orqali kunlik hisobot beradi; mashinachilar MES/IoT avto-o'lchanadi

2. **Kunlik fakt yozish (ckp_fact_values jadval):**
   - card_id, employee_id, product_id, fact_date, actual_value, target_value, achievement_pct (bajarilgan %), formula_type, status, submitted_at
   - Achievement formula: boolean → 100/0; quantity_pct → actual/target × 100
   - Norma NULL → soxta yo'q, faqat egasi norma bersa hisob qilinadi (FABRIKATSIYA TAQIQ)

3. **Kaskad-agregat (cascade aggregate):**
   - Karta + farzandlar (bo'lim/otdeleniye) birgalikda o'rtacha achievement hisoblansa alohida achievement_pct
   - Tugun o'rtacha = o'zining + barcha farzandlarining o'rtachasi

4. **Kunlik hisobot deadline (ЦКП-013 ziddiyat):**
   - Spec PHASE-05: `ckp_report_deadline_hours = 16` soat (kun oxiri 16 soat)
   - OCHIQ-JAVOBLAR: "3 soat" aytildi (3 soatda yubormasa kun-oylik yo'q)
   - **BUZILISH:** Ikkita raqam ziddiyatli, egasi yakuniy soruvdan keyin belgiladi

### Oqim / Integratsiya - `PARTIAL` 22%
- **Vizyon kutadi:** EGASI VIZYONI (DIGEST bo'yicha): 
1. KARTA-MARKAZLI OYLIK: Har xodimning oylik manbai YAGONA = uning PRIMARY kartas'dagi salary_type/min_salary/max_salary/razryad_level_id → oylik hisob qilinadi. Oylik baseSalary param emas, KARTADAN keladi.
2. Card ID→User Login/Oylik GATE: users.card_id NULL bo'lsa login+oylik yo'q (vizyonning markaziy printsipi "kartasiz ERP yo'q"). RBAC/darslik-gate/ЦКП-gate HAMMASI kartadan keladi.
3. Employee Cards M:N + Primary: employee_cards jadval 1:M xodim↔karta, isPrimary bayroq = qaysi karta oylik manbasi. Ko'p karta = oylik yig'indi (stavka-ulush).
4. Razryad→Oylik Koeffitsienti: razryad o'sish (imtihon→HR+rahbar tasdiq) → karta razryad_level_id o'zgaradi → payroll koeffitsienti dinamik o'zgaradi. Razryad o'sish/pasayish zanjiri (EXECUTION) to'liq jonli.
5. GOLDEN-THREAD: SD→PP→MES→QC→WMS→FIN arasidagi zanjir bitta kanonik jadvalga tayanadi (oylik = financial_entries ichidan employee salary part). Oylik→FIN atomik link.
6. INTEGRATSIYA NUQTALARI:

- **Vizyon kutadi:** 
EGASI VIZYONI (DIGEST + MASTER-REJA):

1. **KARTA-MARKAZLI ASOSIY PRINSIP (Q-40 eng muhim):**
   - Karta = ERP'ning MARKAZIY master-datasi; hammasi kartadan keladi
   - `card_id` NULL bo'lsa: login TAQIQ, oylik TAQIQ, RBAC TAQIQ
   - Karta asosiy, xodim ikkilamchi — xodim KO'P kartaga ega bo'lishi mumkin
   - Oylik = biriktirilgan kartalar oyliklari YIGINDISI (stavka-ulush majburiy, cap 1.0)

2. **BITTA XODIM → KO'P KARTA (M:N):**
   - `employee_cards` M:N link: (employee_id, card_id, is_primary, is_acting, acting_supplement, ended_at, ...)
   - Profilda HAMMA kartalar ko'rinadi + oylik YIGINDISI
   - Yangi xodim bosqichli faollashuvi: taqdim→o'qish(2-oy)→imtihon→rahbar tasdiq→full oylik
   - Karta o'zgarsa (razryad/oylik/status) — hamma xodim oyligi qayta hisoblansa

3. **LOGIN-RBAC GATE (EP-ORG-003):**
   - Parolni tekshirgandan KEYIN: `card_id` NULL bo'lsa login taqiq (xodim kartaga biriktirilmagan)
   - Xodim kartasiz faqat BYPASS ROLLAR (super_admin/admin/director) kiradi
   - RBAC = karta FK (karta→razryad→oylik→ruxsat), eski positions'dan EMAS

4. **OYLIK HISOBLANISHI (EP-ORG-142 + darslik-gate):**
   - Oylik formula: employee_id → employee_cards → card_id → org_departments.max_salary SUM
   - DarsLMS gate: darslik tugamasa oylik TOP — gate ulanmagan
   - ЦКП gate: kunlik hisobot deadline → oylik gate (deadline o'tmasa oylik tog'ri HISOB qilinmaydi)

5. **RAZRYAD-OYLIK ZANJIR:**

### To'liqlik - `PARTIAL` 35%
- **Vizyon kutadi:** Digest'ga asosan EuroPrint ERPda org-struktura KARTA-MARKAZLI bo'lishi kerak: (1) 1 karta = 1 o'rindiq (atomik); (2) xodim ko'p karta egallasa oylik = SUM(karta oyliklarining nisbatlari), nisbatlar ≤100%; (3) kartasiz login+oylik TAQIQ (head_user_id gate); (4) vertikal boshqaruv = manager_id (parent_id daraxt); (5) darslik → oylik gate (darslik tugamasa o'sha karta oylik YO'Q); (6) ЦКП kartaga biriktiriladi (xodimga emas), kunlik deadline; (7) soft-delete + audit-tarix; (8) 5-holat FSM (Faol/Vakansiya/I.o./Muzlatilgan/Arxiv); (9) razryad → talab → o'sish → oylik zanjiri; (10) AI baho + karta↔xodim moslik (fit score); (11) Vysotskiy-7 daraxt model; (12) head_user_id yoziladi + read qilinadi (CC marshrut, RBAC, oylik manbai)
- **Qurilgan:** JONLI KOD + DB TASDIQ: (1) Schema: `org_departments` (144 yozuv), `head_user_id INTEGER` ustun tavkir LEKIN **hammasida NULL**. (2) Xodim-karta: `employees.position_id` FK → 30 xodim, hammasi position'ga biriktirilgan. AMMO `employee_org_departments` M:N JUNCTION jadvali MAVJUD (30 yozuv) — 1 xodim ko'p karta biriktirilishi mumkin. (3) Oylik: `org_departments.min_salary/max_salary` ustunlar **NULL** (hech qanday salary qiymati YO'Q). `employee_org_departments` → oylik aggregation (0.5+0.5 sum) logic ABSENT. (4) head_user_id gate: `resolveGate()` method bor (card.service.ts:141-151) ammo NON-BLOCKING flag QAYTARADI, hard block YO'Q. Login/oylik `head_user_id` orqali GATE QILINMAYDI. (5) Darslik gate: LMS/courses jadval + Card linkage logic **YO'Q**. (6) ЦКП: `org_node_portret` JSONB table, upsert ishlaydi LEKIN kunlik deadline SPEC'DA ZIDDIYAT (16h vs 3h). (7) Soft-delete: `is_active` + `deleted_at` + WHERE clauses ✓. (8) FSM: `current_state` enum ✓. (9) Razryad: `razryad_levels` tola, app-layer deterministic ✓. (10) AI fit: `computeCardFit()` 50-50 aggregate ✓. (11) Daraxt: `parent_id` recursion + cycle-guard ✓. (12) head_user_id write: **HECH QAERDA WRITE QILINMAYDI** — INSERT/UPDATE kodi yo'q; faqat SELECT (CC resolver, compat'da READ)
- **Chetlashishlar:**
  - **[P0]** Atomik qoida BUZILGAN: 1 karta ≠ 1 o'rindiq. employee_org_departments M:N ruxsat beradi; service'da 'some cards already hold 3 employees' (EP-ORG-002 comment); App-layer guard deferred until EP-ORG-037 future - _vizyon:_ 1 karta = 1 o'rindiq (atomik). Xodim ko'p karta egallasa ≠ 1 karta ko'p xodimni egallashi _/ build:_ employee_org_departments junction jadvali (30 yozuv); card.service.ts line 49-51 'some cards already hold 3 employees'; canAssignEmployee() app-layer guard (DB UNIQUE index deferred); M:N qurilgan
  - **[P0]** head_user_id (xodim-karta link) MISSING yozish logikasi. Ustun schema'da bor, barcha kartalar NULL. ## 5. Auth / RBAC / login (karta-gate) - o'rtacha 37% (6 tahlil)

### Asosiy g'oya / Model - `PARTIAL` 32%
- **Vizyon kutadi:** The vision requires a card-centric RBAC model where every user must be linked to an active organizational card (org_departments), and login, salary calculation, and permission resolution must all flow from the card as a single source of truth. Specific vision requirements: (1) card_id NULL → login + salary blocked (major principle); (2) RBAC derived from card (rbac_tier primary, razryad-level fallback); (3) Permissions keyed to org_departments (card), not legacy positions; (4) Multiple cards per user supported (stavka ulushi), salary aggregated from all active cards; (5) Card-to-xodim binding via employee_cards (1:M relationship, atomic, not merge/split).
- **Qurilgan:** Live code + DB show hybrid model (double-world): (1) users.employee_id → employee_cards (0 active rows) → org_departments (card model live, 144 rows). (2) users.org_function_id (30/31 users) → org_functions (97 rows, legacy). (3) users.position_id (legacy positions table, 1380 position_permissions rows). Login resolves card via employee_cards query; BUT card-gate default-disabled (CARD_LOGIN_GATE_ENABLED='false'). RBAC permission check keyed to users.position_id (legacy positions), not org_departments. Payload JWT: cardId, rbacTier sent but not enforced — guard accepts position-level RBAC ('PERMISSION_KEY' extracts positionId from JWT, permission.guard.ts:49 queries position_permissions, never touches org_departments.rbac_tier). Salary calculation (not examined but referenced in docs) still reads from org_functions baseSalary, not org_departments card fields. GATE 0 active cards, but no blocking (CARD_LOGIN_GATE_ENABLED env var off by default, regress-safe).
- **Chetlashishlar:**
  - **[P0]** Card-gate default-OFF, not enforced at login - _vizyon:_ card_id NULL → login blocked (global principle) _/ build:_ login.service.ts:126 `CARD_LOGIN_GATE_ENABLED='false'` env-gated; isCardExemptRole() bypass (super_admin/admin/director); gate.activeCardCount=0 but no blocking if env off (regress-safe, but unwired)
  - **[P0]** RBAC keyed to position_id (legacy), not card - _vizyon:_ permissions derived from org_departments (card), single source _/ build:_ permission.guard.ts:49 queries position_permissions (module:level) by positionId; drizzle-my-permissions.repo.ts:54-68 JOINs users→positions→org_functions for tier (org_functions.rbac_tier fallback), never org_departments.rbac_tier. Position data 1380 rows, card model separate.
  - **[P1]** employee_cards all inactive (0 rows is_active=true) - _vizyon:_ user-card binding via active employee_cards; card_id primary key in jwt + guard ruxsat o'qishi _/ build:_ resolveCardGate (drizzle-auth.repo.ts:224) queried employee_cards, returns 0 active cards for all users. Users bound to legacy org_function_id (30/31 with value). FE org-structure uses org_departments (144 rows), but users not linked.
  - **[P1]** JWT payload carries cardId, rbacTier but permission-guard ignores card - _vizyon:_ cardId→org_departments.rbac_tier resolution flow, card-first logic _/ build:_ login.service.ts:210 embeds cardId/rbacTier in JWT payload; but permission.guard.ts never reads them — uses positionId only. Derive-on-read (rbac-tier.policy.ts) logic present but dormant.
  - **[P2]** Salary calculation entry point unknown (not in auth module) - _vizyon:_ salary_payroll must read org_departments card (salary_type, max_salary, bonus_config, razryad coeff) _/ build:_ Docs say 'calculate-payroll.handler baseSalary param'da hisoblaydi' (legacy), not card-sourced. Auth module does not wire salary.
- **Isbot:** `drizzle-auth.repo.ts:224-261: resolveCardGate() queried employee_cards (0 active), returned empty activeCardCount for all users` - `login.service.ts:121-132: card-gate env-flagged CARD_LOGIN_GATE_ENABLED, guard.activeCardCount===0 but no block if env='false' (default)` - `login.service.ts:204-214: JWT payload {cardId, rbacTier, positionId} assembled, sent to FE` - `permission.guard.ts:63-81: canActivate() reads positionId from user JWT, queries position_permissions, never touches org_departments; position_id fallback line 69`

### To'liqlik - `PARTIAL` 28%
- **Vizyon kutadi:** EGASINING VIZYONI (DIGEST-asosida, docs/):

1. **CARD_ID NULL → LOGIN YO'Q + OYLIK YO'Q** (EP-ORG-003, Q-03): Kartasiz (card_id NULL) xodim ERPga kira olmaydi; Payroll oylik hisoblashdan skip. Karta = login/oylik/RBAC manba.

2. **RBAC KARTADAN** (EP-ORG-023, Q-23): Ko'rish/qilish/tasdiq huquqlari kartadan keladi. Karta o'zgarsa ruxsat o'zgaradi. Position-based EMAS — karta-based RBAC.

3. **Login Guardlar**: JWT (token verify + blacklist) + Roles (rol-asosida bypass) + Permission (position_permissions modulga-level) + SOD (sod.guard) — 4 ta global guard.

4. **Token/JWT Maxfiyligi**: HS256 + JWT_REFRESH_SECRET alohida + bcrypt×12 parol; hech qachon default parol/hardcoded credential.

5. **Kartaga bog'lanish MAJBURIY**: card_id ustuni users jadvalida bo'lishi va NULL-check  login/payroll-servicelarda bo'lishi kerak (karta biriktirilmagan xodim ERPga kira olmaydi, oyligini hisoblash taqiq).

6. **Field-level RBAC** (EP-ORG-042): Maxfiy maydonlar (oylik/AI-baho/razryad-tarix) faqat ruxsatli kartalarga BE projection'da ko'rinadi (FE emas).

7. **I.o. Scoped Delegation** (EP-ORG-062, Q-62): I.o. (acting) rejimida kunlik operatsiya-ha/pul-kadr-yo'q ajratuvchi RBAC qatlami. 

8. **2-Imzo Karta Faollashtiruvi** (EP-ORG-126, Q-17, Q-591): Karta 2 raqamli imzo (RD + tanishgan xodim) bilan kuchga kiradi; onboarding tugaganda avto-faol.

9. **Dir​ektor Yagona Tasdiqlovchi** (GLOBAL PRINTSIP): Tasdiq oxiri organizatsiya-sxemasida direktorga, summa-tier EMAS (kuchli markaziy nazorat).

10. **Global Adolat**: AI kuzatadi/belgilaydi (bahalar, kech-kelish, brak), LEKIN salbiy ta'sir (jarima/ball/blok/razryad-pasayish) FAQAT inson tasdig'idan o'tadi (avto-jarima TAQIQ).

### UI / UX - `PARTIAL` 45%
- **Vizyon kutadi:** **Egasi vizyoni (FASE-2 masterreja, EP-ORG-003 / EP-ORG-023 / EP-ORG-042 / EP-ORG-103):** Login sahifasida foydalanuvchi standart shakl (username/parol) to'ldiradi. Mazmun: (1) KARTA-GATE: kartasiz user → 401 "lavozim kartasiga biriktirilmagansiz" xatosi, login BLOK qilinadi. ISTISNO: super_admin/admin/director (tizim-darajasi rollar) → kartasiz ham login mumkin (regress-himoya). (2) JWT PROPAGATION: login muvaffaqiyatli bo'lsa, token cardId/rbacTier/positionId propagate qiladi (permission.guard ularni o'qiydi). (3) SESSION CONTINUITY: karta o'zgarsa (sessiya davomida ko'chsa/o'chsa), har request jwt.strategy aktiv-kartani re-check qiladi; kartasiz bo'lsa 401 bilan 401 unauthorized qaytaradi (tizim-darajasi bypass qoladi). (4) RBAC MANBAI: ruxsat `org_departments.rbac_tier` (kartadan) + `position_permissions` (positionId dan). (5) PAYROLL GATE: aktiv-kartasiz xodim oyligi hisobdan SKIP qilinadi. **FE UX ASPECT:** Login forma izchil (floating labels, error toast, loading state), error xabari (401→"kartaga biriktirilmagansiz", 5xx→"server xatosi", network→"ulanish muammosi") — foydalanuvchi bilsa kartasiz login mumkin emas. Ombor operatori → POS Monitor, kassir naqd operatsiyalar → to'liq ulanmagan HOZIR. **Muhim:** vizyonda KARTA = markaziy, xodim ikkilamchi. Admin exception bo'lsa ham, oddiy xodim kartasiz ERPga kira olmaydi.
- **Qurilgan:** **JONLI TASDIQLANMALAR (2026-06-25 DB+kod):** ✅ SEBATI QURILGAN: (1) CardGate interfeys + resolveCardGate() repo metodi MAVJUD (i-auth.repo.ts, drizzle-auth.repo.ts) — active_cards soni, primaryCardId, rbacTier, positionId qaytaradi. (2) Login.service: resolveCardGate() chaqiriladi, LEKIN FEATURE FLAG `CARD_LOGIN_GATE_ENABLED='true'` talab qiladi (env-var, default OFF) — "bugun login buzilishiga xavf qilmaslik uchun" flaglash o'chiq qilingan. Flag OFF bo'lsa gate hisoblanjr, JWT'ga claim qo'shiladi, LEKIN 401 BLOK qilinmaydi. (3) JWT payload: cardId/rbacTier/positionId qo'shildi (MASA-0...140 active_cards='0', hozir SELECT ko'rsatadi — hech kim kartaga biriktirilmagan). (4) i18n.auth.noActiveCard = "Siz hech qaysi lavozim kartasiga biriktirilmagansiz..." MAVJUD (uz/ru/uz-cyr). (5) permission.guard: user.positionId ?? user['positionId'] o'qiydi (jwt token'dan keladi). (6) RBAC tier manbasini KARTAga ko'chishni (org_departments.rbac_tier + LATERAL subquery) 'EM TAYYORLANMAGAN — faqat positions.rbac_tier qo'shni HOZIR ishlatadi. (7) Payroll SKIP: aktiv-karta tekshiruv payroll.service'da TAVSIFLANGAN (direktiva FAZA-02 bosqich-6), LEKIN KODNI IMPLEMENT QILINMAGAN. (8) JWT.strategy: sessiya davomida karta re-check TAVSIFLANGAN, LEKIN IMPLEMENT QILINMAGAN — faqat login da. ✅ FE LOGIN UX: Login.tsx standart shakl (floating label FloatingInput), error handling (401/429/500+), loading state EPLoader, xato toast (Zod validation, HTTP status sortirovka). ## 6. PP / ishlab-chiqarish reja - o'rtacha 39% (5 tahlil)

### Asosiy g'oya / Model - `PARTIAL` 42%
- **Vizyon kutadi:** PP (Production Planning) module should embody two core architectural principles from owner vision: (1) CARD-CENTRIC MODEL: every work center, planner, and responsible role traces back to org_card (KARTA) — cards are the source of truth for hierarchy, malaka/razryad, GSD/ЦКП, and oylik. ProductionOrder→plannedManagerId→card; workCenter→orgDepartmentId→card; operator→card. (2) MULTI-LEVEL PLANNING (Vysotskiy-7 hierarchy): Planning authority = **vertikal marshrut orga:** all critical decisions (freeze, replan, urgent insert, capacity override) escalate to the department head → upward through org-sxema to direktor. Planning must respect Vysotskiy-7 (7-level tree): zavodga→5-otdeleniye→3-bo'lim→3-sektsiya→3-sektor. NO parallel "planning department" entity — planning IS a card role within the 5-otdeleniye. Golden-thread intact: SD→PP→MES→QC→WMS→FIN with real DB inserts, event broadcasts (pp.released), cancellation parity. Status lifecycle per EP-PP-082: 7 forward (Reja→Tasdiqlangan→Ishga tushgan→Jarayonda→Sifatda→Tugadi→Yopildi) + 2 terminal (Bekor/To'xtatilgan); all transitions journaled. AI-rejalashtirish (EP-PP-003): AI taklif → human tasdiq, never auto-execute. Muzlatilgan zona (EP-PP-025): ~3 kun near-term replan-proof. ЦКП haraka (EP-PP-028): "Reja bajarilish %"=rejalashtirish card's KPI source.
- **Qurilgan:** Current build: (1) **CARD-LINKAGE WEAK**: workCenters has `orgDepartmentId` column (nullable, Wave 4 TBD, Q-40 data pending) BUT productionOrders has NO card reference whatsoever. Production order flows: creator+responsible roles (`responsibleManagerId`, `shiftSupervisorId`, `qcInspectorId`) are VARCHAR (50-char strings), NOT integer card IDs; no FK to org_cards. Operator assignment (`operatorId` on productionOrderOperations) is integer but references users, not cards. (2) **PLANNING AUTHORITY STRUCTURAL FLAW**: No vertikal marshrut → no escalation. Replan/freeze/urgent decisions hard-coded logic-only (not role-gated via card-hierarchy). Rejalashtirish bo'lim = separate functional team (bosh rejalashtirish = card role, but code treats reja as data-only, not governance). (3) **STATUS LIFECYCLE**: PoStatus enum correctly implements EP-PP-082 (7+2 statuses), transitions allowed. (4) **GOLDEN-THREAD**: SD→PP intact via `salesOrderId` FK + `PpReleasedEvent`. But MES→QC integration unclear (QC gate logic not visible in PP schema; likely deferred to QC module). (5) **AI-REJALASHTIRISH**: No AI layer visible; PP README mentions `pp-intelligence.service` (MRP orchestrator) but no AI/suggestion endpoint. (6) **ЦКП SOURCE**: PP does NOT popula te card ЦКП metrics (no column-level link to ЦКП calculation). Norma+deadline on tech-card but aggregation to card-GSD deferred (EP-PP-028). **ARTIFACTS:** `lib/db/src/schema/pp/pp-production.ts:461–524` (productionOrders DDL, lacking card foreign key); `apps/api/src/modules/pp/domain/aggregates/production-order.aggregate.ts:83–120` (lifecycle enum+transitions OK, but no org-hierarchy injection); `apps/api/src/modules/pp/README.md` (MRP pipeline documented, MES juncture clear, AI absent from design).
- **Chetlashishlar:**
  - **[P0]** productionOrders lacks card_id (org_card FK) — responsible role identification is VARCHAR string, not card reference; no vertikal marshrut governance - _vizyon:_ Every production order: plannedManagerId (responsible role) = card ID integer, with FK to org_cards. All replan/freeze/urgent authorization via card → org-hierarchy escalation. Manager role = card attribute (karta.lavozim='Bosh rejalashtirish' → 5-otdeleniye ва yuqoriga) (EP-PP-001/002/022/081 governance). _/ build:_ productionOrders.responsibleManagerId = VARCHAR(50) no FK; ownership transfer via string name match risk. workCenters.orgDepartmentId present but NULLABLE, data pending Q-40. No role-hierarchy gate logic for replan/freeze (hard-coded checks only).
  - **[P0]** workCenter operatorId/supervisorId assignment uses users.id, not card — breaks karta-markaz principle; operator malaka (razryad/sertifikat) NOT checked at assign time - _vizyon:_ Operator assignment (productionOrderOperations.operatorId) = card ID. Card has razryad+LMS sertifikat. Before assign: system checks karta.razryad ≥ operation.requiredLevel + sertifikat completion (EP-PP-018, EP-PP-038, workCenters.certificationLmsCourseId check). _/ build:_ operatorId:integer→users.id (not cards). No pre-assign validation. workCenters has certificationLmsCourseId+requiredSkillName columns but assign handler likely absent (EP-PP-018 OPEN). users.id ≠ card_id fundamental mismatch.
  - **[P0]** AI-rejalashtirish (EP-PP-003, EP-PP-130..136) = MISSING — no suggestion/approval endpoint; MRP runner blind - _vizyon:_ AI suggests reja (navbat/marshrut/ustuvorlik/gang-run), human tahrirlaydi+tasdiqlaydi. AI reachability = 'PP/AI' section (Aisha or dedicated planning-AI). Suggestion rationale visible (EP-PP-031 'sababi: 2-stanok bo'sh'). _/ build:_ pp-intelligence.service documented as MRP orchestrator, but no AI layer. pp.module imports no AI sub-module. PP README §How a Sales Order pulls MRP (§4.2) silent on AI. Suggests MRP is DETERMINISTIC (L4L/EOQ/POQ/Wagner-Whitin), not AI-guided.

But NO column-link (gsd_value_this_month, gsd_formula_type) on cards ⟷ PP. Rejalashtirish GSD likely manual-entry or deferred to Director module.
  - **[P1]** Replan/Freeze/Urgent governance = HARD-CODED LOGIC, not ROLE-GATED — no escalation pathway - _vizyon:_ Replan (EP-PP-022): only bosh rejalashtirish or higher (vertikal up to direktor). Freeze (EP-PP-025): only direktor/egasi. Urgent (EP-PP-030): tizim taklif qiladi, bosh rejalashtirish tasdiqlaydi. All decisions RECORDED in org-marshrut audit (COR-089 protokol). _/ build:_ productionOrders.isFrozen, isUrgent booleans; no role check. No vertikal escalation logic. ### To'liqlik - `PARTIAL` 42%
- **Vizyon kutadi:** **PP = T1 OLTIN-IP YADROSI** — SD→PP→MES→QC→WMS→FIN eng muhim zanjirning markaziy bog'lovchisi. Vizyon talab qiladi: (1) **7-holatli production_orders aylanishi** (Reja→Tasdiqlangan→Ishga tushgan→Jarayonda→Sifatda→Tugadi→Yopildi + Bekor/To'xtatilgan), (2) **Texkarta master-data** (BOM/marshrut/versiyalash/lab-gate/6-element speksifikatsiya), (3) **AI rejalashtiruvchi** 7-bosqich (buyurtma→material→bron→marshrut→vaqt→reja→ijro), (4) **MRP kamomad + material bron** (warehouse_stock bilan mos), (5) **CRP quvvat yu klanishi** (22+ stanok, 2 smena), (6) **Smena-gantt + 4-raqam plan-fakt kiritish** (reja/fakt/qolgan/brak + 5-sabab kodi guruhi), (7) **Muzlatilgan zona** (~3 kun, egasi/direktor ruxsati bilan o'zgartirish), (8) **Bitta kanonik jadval** (production_orders, warehouse_stock, entries), (9) **Oltin-ip event zanjiri** (19 event, outbox, stateless). Yagona o'lchov = master vizyon (`docs/XARITA-REJA-YONALISH`, `decisions/07-pp.md` 136 savol/tasdiqlanish, `OCHIQ-JAVOBLAR`): kod vizyon qoidalari bilan To'G'RI mos ishlaysa = MATCH, zid ishlasa (ishlasa ham) = DIVERGENT, yo'q bo'lsa = MISSING, soxta/echo/hardcode = FAKE.

Oltin-ip event-chain (sales-order-ready-planning.listener, advance-approved.listener) mavjud lekin EP-PP-025 (frozen-until) serve logic YO'Q. **AI tafsifi**: pp-intelligence.service.ts mavjud, lekin 7-bosqich zanjir (material→bron→marshrut→vaqt) mos asosdan bo'lmagani uchun taklif soxta (hardcoded). **Natijai**: Kod "ishlaydi" (tashkil darajasida SELECT qiladi, 200 qaytaradi), lekin vizyonga nisbatan ~45% to'liq — struktura, qoida-logika, data-gate, event, master-data (5-sabab, frozen-days, status-log) — QISMIY/SOXTA/YO'Q.

### Oqim / Integratsiya - `PARTIAL` 35%
- **Vizyon kutadi:** Egasi vizyoni (DIGEST + XARITA-REJA-YONALISH): (1) Golden-thread SD→PP→MES→QC→WMS→FIN bitta uzluksiz zanjir; SD'da buyurtma confirmed (ready_for_planning) bo'lsa PP avtomatik qo'l oladi, production_order yaratadi, MES'ga jo'natadi. (2) Event-driven: OrderStatusChangedEvent (SD) → SalesOrderReadyPlanningListener (PP) → createPlanFromSalesOrder → PpReleasedEvent → MES; async parallel channels. (3) Kanonik jadvallar: sales_orders + production_orders + production_sessions FK-bog'langan, ikki-olam yo'q. (4) AI rejalashtirish (MRP/MPS/CRP/Johnson) LIVE algoritmlar qurilgan. (5) Status mashinasi: 7+ holat (created→planned→confirmed→released→in_progress→completed) real transition guard + event emitter.
- **Qurilgan:** QURILGAN: (1) pp.module.ts — 8+ controller, 19+ CQRS handler, 6 event listener; SalesOrderReadyPlanningListener.ts REAL: CQRS OrderStatusChangedEvent eshitadi, ppRepo.createPlanFromSalesOrder chaqiradi. (2) Schema: production_orders (pp-production.ts:461-524) sales_order_id FK → sales_orders bor; work_centers, routing, BOM, MRP jadvallar qurilgan. (3) execSavePo() queries-pp.ts:13-44 production_orders INSERT yozadi. (4) PpReleasedEvent, ApproveRoutingHandler kodlangan. BUZILGANLAR: (1) PP→MES listener TOPILMADI — MES PpReleasedEvent eshitmaydi; PP→MES data-flow silent. (2) production_orders.org_department_id ustuni SCHEMA'DA YO'Q — reja kartaga bog'lanmaydi. (3) AI rejalashtirish dangsiz — MRP/MPS/CRP algoritmlar qurilgan lekin DATABASE EMPTY (production_orders=0, work_centers=0, routings=0); nma rejalashtiradi? Hech narsa. (4) production_order_components INSERT listener yo'q — WMS issue amali trigger qilinmaydi. (5) Status transition guard qay o'tish allowed deb implement qilinmagan. (6) Karta-bog'lanish yo'q: Reja MES'ga jo'natsa ham org_departments (kim ishlay) malumoti yo'q.

Real sales order (56, confirmed) → Production order (48) → Session (35) → QC (34) → Stock → GL. domain_events = 0 qator (outbox qurilgan lekin hech qanday event saqlanmagan).

Real test order 56 (confirmed) → production_order (48) → session (35) → qc_inspection (34) → warehouse_stock bor. ## 8. IoT / telemetriya - o'rtacha 43% (7 tahlil)

### Asosiy g'oya / Model - `PARTIAL` 62%
- **Vizyon kutadi:** VIZYON (docs/audit/MASSIV-100/00-MASTER-REJA.md, FAZA-MES-016, decisions/08-mes.md): MES = xodimning (KARTA-markazli) production-session'larini kuzatadi. Har MES sessiya: operator (org_departments card_id'si orqali, xodim emas) → work_center (mashina) → production_order (PP dan) → session status → OEE → GSD kartasiga write. AKAT SIYOSATI: (1) operator = kartaga bog'lanmagan xodim — bu XATOLIK (xodim = profilga yig'indi; sessiya operator → karta → oylik → GSD); (2) produktion sessions = production_sessions jadvalga real INSERT (echo/fake TAQIQ); (3) Downtime kuzatiladigan: downtime_events + downtime_reason_codes (master data bunyodor); (4) OEE hisob = Availability × Performance × Quality (0-100 scale, world-standard); (5) Sessiya OEE-natijasi → karta GSD'siga yoziladi (EP-MES-019); (6) IoT yo'q — qo'lda operator kiritadi (EP-MES-080); (7) Smena = A/B/C nomi, reja-formasida jonli tablo "operatоr → mashina" (А смена План.xlsx haqiqiy forma); (8) Shift handover = rasmiy handover jadval (shift_handovers kanonik); (9) Checklist gate (TB safety/smena readiness) → session READY→CHECKLIST_PENDING→RUNNING (EP-HR-079/COR-130); (10) Material sarf = avto-hisob + operator tasdiqi (norma bo'yicha); (11) 3 bosqich session (hop3): tayyorgarlik (настройка) → asosiy → yakunlash (EP-MES-001); (12) Operator × mashina malaka matritsasi (qaysi mashinada ishlay oladi) — real RBAC (EP-MES-054).
- **Qurilgan:** JONLI KOD (apps/api/src/modules/mes + lib/db/src/schema/pp/pp-iot.ts):
— `production_sessions` jadval (pp-iot.ts:51-89): ✅ KANONIK; ustunlar: productionOrderId, equipmentId, workerId (worker_id → users.id), status (pending/running/paused/stopped/completed), actualQuantity, defectQuantity, oee/availability/performance/quality. ✅ Real DB table; haqqiqiy INSERT'lar bor.
— `downtime_events` jadval (pp-iot.ts:113-135): ✅ KANONIK; FK sessionId → production_sessions.id; eventType (auto_detected/manual_entry); reasonCode (material_shortage/machine_breakdown/setup/maintenance/break/other — **11 KODI**); isPlanned flag; durationSeconds hisob qilingan. ✅ MASTER DATA kanonik.
— `downtimeReasonCodes` jadval (pp-iot.ts:201-222): ✅ KANONIK; kod+nom+kategoriya (planned/unplanned/external); isActive flag.
— `ProductionSession` aggregate (domain/aggregates/production-session.aggregate.ts): ✅ DDD model; state-machine (READY → CHECKLIST_PENDING → RUNNING → PAUSED → COMPLETED → SENT_TO_QC); passChecklist() gate (EP-HR-079 checklist enforcement); recordDowntime() + downtime array; moveToQc() event.
— OEE hisob (README.md README: 85-110): ✅ JONLI FORMULA — Availability × Performance × Quality (0-100 scale, tidak rata-rata naive; volume-weighted). ✅ Hisob'dan kesh (1-min cache) + real DB aggregate (SUM).
— Shift handovers: ✅ `shift_handovers` jadval (hr-performance-ext.ts) — KANONIK; status (pending/acknowledged/completed).

BRAK 1: XODIM vs KARTA ziddiyati
— workerId → users.id (xodim), LEKIN vizyon talab qiladi: operator = KARTA (org_departments.id). ### To'liqlik - `PARTIAL` 38%
- **Vizyon kutadi:** VISION: 50 point MES specification (docs/audit/vision-1000-answers/08-mes.md). Core: (1) operator IoT-tablet markazig (brak, TB-checklist, downtime, priladka, material scanning); (2) 3-stage production session (setup/main/finalization with separate timing per stage); (3) OEE formula separating setup-time (correcting broken old 1-time model); (4) 3-tier escalation for 15min/30min/60min downtime to supervisor/director/SOS; (5) shift handover requiring double-signature, no-signature blocks everything; (6) defect reason triggers QC+MM root-cause event; (7) GSD tallied per-stage by department (not global); (8) norms changeable by НО-manager but only for NEXT shifts (immutable history); (9) AI auto-summary approvals; (10) GSD daily deadline blocks payroll if missed.
- **Qurilgan:** ACTUAL BUILD (code+DB verified): (1) IoT tablet module EXISTS (iot-tablet.controller.ts + iot-tablet.schemas.ts) with crew assignment (POST endpoint pending P15 fix). (2) production_sessions table has only 1 status field (active/stopped/completed) — NO 3-stage lifecycle columns (sozlash/asosiy/yakunlash timing MISSING). (3) OEE snapshot exists (oeeSnapshots table: availability/performance/quality/oee fields) but setup-time adjustment logic MISSING from handlers. (4) downtime_events table and escalation logic EXISTS (15/30/60 min thresholds in vision, repo shows recording/finding) but SOS org-chart routing handler NOT IMPLEMENTED. (5) shift_handovers table EXISTS (mesShiftHandovers: outgoing/incoming supervisor, notes, pending tasks) but DOUBLE-SIGNATURE VALIDATION CODE MISSING. (6) defect logging in worker_session_events exists but QC+MM event triggering INCOMPLETE. (7) GSD cascading per-department DESIGN exists (EP-MES-019) but NOT OPERATIONALIZED in code. (8) mesMaintenance service shows weighting storage but NEXT-shift-only enforcement MISSING. (9) AI summary generation planned (DEFERRED to Phase via docs). (10) GSD daily deadline gate MISSING from payroll integration. SCHEMAS: 7 tables (mesTasks, mesPapkaOrders, mesShiftHandovers, mesShiftEvaluations, mesMaintenanceTasks, mesProductionSessions, mesShiftStats + IoT's productionSessions/downtimeEvents/oeeSnapshots/workerSessionEvents). CODE FILES: 45 files (commands/handlers/repos/services/controllers). VISIBLE STATUS: Partial skeleton with stub/echo returns.
- **Chetlashishlar:**
  - **[P0]** 3-stage session lifecycle (sozlash/asosiy/yakunlash) MISSING: production_sessions table has only status VARCHAR (active/stopped/completed) — NO stage, sozlash_started_at, sozlash_ended_at, asosiy_started_at, yakunlash_started_at/ended_at columns. Vision EP-MES-001 requires 3 bosqich with separate timing. Code treats entire session as 1 stage (bug). - _vizyon:_ production_sessions.stage VARCHAR(20), .sozlash_started_at TIMESTAMPTZ, .sozlash_ended_at, .asosiy_started_at, .yakunlash_started_at, .yakunlash_ended_at (immutable per vision point #17: operator cannot retro-enter plan data). _/ build:_ Only: status, startTime, endTime, producedQty, defectQty (pp-iot.ts:51-86). OEE handler cannot separate setup-time from running-time. completeSessionHandler (commands/complete-session.handler.ts) returns void/stub — does not update stage progression.
  - **[P0]** OEE formula setup-time adjustment MISSING: Vision EP-MES-014 requires Availability = (running_time - downtime) / (planned_production_time - setup_time). Hozir: oeeSnapshots stores calculated OEE but calcOee logic does NOT subtract setup-time. Old 1-stage model persists (broken per vision). - _vizyon:_ OEE calculation must split: Availability = (ActualRunningTime - UnplannedDowntime) / (PlannedTime - SozlashTime). Separate sozlash and main stages. _/ build:_ oeeSnapshots table has availability FIELD but no formula in code. get-oee.handler.ts is STUB (exports empty GetOeeQuery, no calculation). mesShiftStats.downtimeMin aggregates but excludes planned setup.
  - **[P0]** 3-tier downtime escalation + SOS org-chart routing (EP-MES-009) MISSING: Vision requires 15min to usta, 30min to direktor, 60min to SOS. No code path for org-chart traversal (manager_id chain) or Telegram push. - _vizyon:_ downtime_events + listener: if durationMinutes >= 15: notify supervisor; >= 30: notify director; >= 60: SOS Telegram. Use org_departments.manager_id chain. _/ build:_ downtime_events table EXISTS (drizzle-downtime.repo.ts finds/records), but NO handler listening to downtime creation. NO org-chart traversal. NO Telegram. PpReleasedMesListener exists but only for PP to MES, not downtime escalation.
  - **[P1]** Shift handover DOUBLE-SIGNATURE validation MISSING (EP-MES-023): mesShiftHandovers stores notes/issues but no signature count or approval-status field. No handler enforcing 2-imzo before payroll closure. - _vizyon:_ mesShiftHandovers: sign_count INT (0/1/2), approval_status VARCHAR (pending/approved). Payroll hook: smena.closed = true ONLY if sign_count = 2. _/ build:_ mesShiftHandovers: id, outgoingSupervisor, incomingSupervisor, notes, handoverTime, pendingTasksCount. NO sign_count, NO approval_status.
  - **[P1]** Defect root-cause event to QC+MM INCOMPLETE (EP-MES-047): worker_session_events records defect_reason but no structured event emission to QC+MM modules. - _vizyon:_ defect_report event fires MesDefectDetected(sessionId, defectQty, reason) to QC+MM listeners for root-cause identification. _/ build:_ workerSessionEvents stores {eventType, defectQuantity, defectReason}. NO domain event firing. NO listener in QC module.
  - **[P1]** GSD per-stage per-department tally NOT OPERATIONALIZED (EP-MES-019): handlers do not disaggregate GSD by department+stage; they sum globally. - _vizyon:_ SessionStageCompleted event fires for each stage/department independently; ORG-GSD listeners tally per-department only. _/ build:_ mesShiftStats aggregates by machineId only, NO department_id column. NO stage-completion events.
  - **[P1]** Norms change NEXT-ONLY enforcement MISSING (EP-MES-029): no versioning with effective_from_date or next-shift validation. - _vizyon:_ Norm update includes effective_shift_id; historical data reads norms@creation_date (immutable); recalc only for future. _/ build:_ NO norm table visible. OEE weights hard-coded. equipment.oee_target exists but no version_date.
  - **[P1]** AI auto-approval gate (EP-MES-079) DEFERRED, not even STUB: No code path for AI summary to usta review to smena close. - _vizyon:_ After 3 stages complete: MesSessionComplete event fires; AI listener generates summary; usta gets notification; tasdig' button closes session. _/ build:_ complete-session.handler.ts returns void (stub). No AI integration. No push notification.
  - **[P2]** GSD daily deadline payroll gate MISSING (ORG-Q111): Vision requires xodim kunlik GSD hisoboti deadline o'tmasa oylik BLOCK. Currently no integration between MES daily-deadline and Payroll oylik closure. - _vizyon:_ kunlik GSD deadline o'tmasa is_gsd_reported = false; Payroll skips oylik for that xodim until submission. _/ build:_ mesMaintenance has reports but not xodim GSD daily submissions. No payroll gate code visible.

### To'liqlik - `PARTIAL` 35%
- **Vizyon kutadi:** **IoT vision (from digest + decisions/16-iot.md + OCHIQ-JAVOBLAR):** 
1. **Andon board (EP-IOT-021)** — katta tablo barcha mashinalarning real-time holati (qizil=to'xtagan, yashil=ishlamoqda, sariq=sozlanmoqda) ≥1-5 sekund jo'natiladi, jonli yangilanadi, tezkor ko'rish (boshliq tepada turmaydi).
2. **Sensor tahlili (EP-IOT-001..009)** — sensoriaz ishlab chiqarish: ≥3-5 asosiy mashina (Gofra liniyasi, KBA-105, SM-72) sensor o'rnatiladi, har bir mashina: uptime%, downtime sabablari (tarh.ro'yxat), anomaliya chegaralari, OEE (3 omil: vaqt+tezlik+sifat), RUL (qolgan resurs).
3. **AI Kamera monitoring** — xona inspeksiyasi har 2 soatda (ideal-rasm bilan AI taqqoslash, delta-E<3=okey), 5-7 mezon (tozalik/himoya/tartib/xavfsizlik), anomaliya→jurnal+tuzatish-plan, xodimlar himoya vositasi nazorati.
4. **IoT-Telegram escalation** — anomaliya/idle→darhol smena rahbari (Telegram+sex ekrani), 10 min javobsiz→sex boshlig'i, 25 min→direktor, tungi smena chegara 20% past.
5. **IoT-MES integratsiya** — sensor→MES avto bajarildi qiymat, downtime auto loglar, OEE snapshot, GSD orqali ЦКП mashina-operatorga.
6. **A-System o'rnini bosish** — eski tizim (A-System) ERP'da toliq almastiriladi, bitta haqiqat manbai (ikki-olam YO'Q).
7. **Operator IoT-tablet** — shift boshlash/tugatish, defekt kiritish, TB checklist, smena topshirish — 3 bosqich (setup→main→close), OEE real-vaqt, Andon jonli.
Status: 37 savol ✅ (AI kamera markaziy: xona inspeksiya, davomat, sifat kamera); 46 savol 🔵 OCHIQ (sensor hali o'rnatilmagan — CAPEX keyin, fazaviy yo'nalishlar).

Migration `iot-remove-fake-sensor-seed-2026-06-22.sql` DEMO sensor'larni o'chirib tashlagan (SNS-* pre-seed 96 o'qish, 6 mashina, soxta RANDOM() qiymatlari — Q-40 buzilishi, tuzatilgan). **Real sensor hali o'rnatilmagan** (egasi tasdiqlanmagan).

### UI / UX - `PARTIAL` 72%
- **Vizyon kutadi:** IoT tablet = floor command center for operators. Operator logs in (tabel+PIN), selects equipment+order, scans material kit, records shifts, reports defects/downtime/QC, signs via digital signature. UI: mobile-optimized, large touch targets, bilingual (UZ/RU) one-tap toggle, real-time status (timer/countdown/QC-reminder), dialog-based workflows (SOS/Defect/Downtime/QC/Handover as modals), color coding (green=running, red=error, yellow=warning). Energy-saving mode (blank after inactivity, tap-wake), auto-stop 30s no-signal, completion report structured. Operator identity: low-literacy/high-speed interaction.
- **Qurilgan:** PRODUCTION CODE EXISTS: FE has 9 tsx files (IoTTablet.tsx entry, IoTLoginPanel, IoTSchedulePanel, IoTProductionDashboard, IoTChecklistModal, IoTCompletionReport, SectionComponents), 7 custom hooks, real mutations via apiRequest. BE: iot-tablet.service (login/SOS), production-sessions controller (start/stop/defect/QC/handover), drizzle repos. Login UI: tabel input numeric-only, h-14 button, UZ/RU toggle. Session state: activeSession object with targetQuantity, actualQuantity, defectQuantity. Dialog modals for SOS/Defect/Downtime/QC/Handover with form fields. Energy overlay: fixed black bg, Moon icon, tap-dismiss. Auto-stop: 30s no-signal threshold. Progress: 4-card metrics grid, barcode, completion report tabs.
- **Chetlashishlar:**
  - **[P1]** Checklist modal detached from session lifecycle - should visually block start until complete per COR-130/HR-079. Vision expects mandatory TB-safety checklist before production start. - _vizyon:_ Session state machine: READY to passChecklist() to CHECKLIST_PENDING to start(). Checklist modal BLOCKING with red overlay if incomplete. HR-079 TB safety items (equipment/material/crew/PPE) must all check before start unlocks. _/ build:_ IoTChecklistModal renders AFTER createSession, material-kit + item scans. Modal is optional (user can skip if no kit). Session already created before checklist shown. Start button no checklist-dependency. Missing TB-safety items.
  - **[P1]** Shift handover form collects 5 fields but no APPROVAL workflow. Vision expects handover to trigger HR 360 degree + MES_COMPLETED event plus move to QC. Currently seems to just INSERT row. - _vizyon:_ Shift handover = critical gate. Form plus PIN sign triggers MesCompletedEvent to HR_360 (AI evaluates) plus QC pickup. Completion report shows QC acknowledged handoff. _/ build:_ IoTCompletionReport Smena topshirish akti with 5 Textarea fields plus signature. submitHandover POSTs endpoint. No visible MES_COMPLETED event handling, QC acknowledgment, or HR 360 result in UI.
  - **[P1]** IoT tablet page routing has TWO sidebar entries (iot/tablet and iot/dashboard) - vision expects SINGLE floor tablet app. Operator vs monitor view unclear. - _vizyon:_ IoT tablet = SINGLE PWA for operators at /iot/tablet. Dashboard monitoring is SEPARATE director view. _/ build:_ Sidebar lists IoT Planshet (/iot/tablet) AND IoT Dashboard (/iot/dashboard). Dual-link confuses operator routing and creates duplicate entry points.
  - **[P2]** Shift countdown shows no ESCALATING urgency when remaining time low. When shift less than 15 minutes, UI should pulse red and block new session start per vision. - _vizyon:_ Visual escalation at 0-5 minutes: red blink plus Smena tugadi warning. Cannot start NEW session if shift remaining less than 10 minutes. Time gate enforced. _/ build:_ DashboardHeader shows shiftRemaining in yellow if remaining greater than 0, red if ended. No session-start gate on time. ActionButtons startSession has no shift-time check.
  - **[P2]** QC reminder popup is optional snooze but vision expects MANDATORY at fixed intervals. No enforced QC sampling gate if deadline passed. - _vizyon:_ CKP kunlik hisobot deadline 3 hours - QC reminder BLOCKS production if deadline passed without submission. Hard gate enforcement. _/ build:_ qcReminderVisible state with QcReminderBanner button. setShowQcFormDialog(true) on click. No deadline timer, no block-if-overdue enforcement logic.
  - **[P2]** Downtime reason codes dropdown exists but unstandardized. Vision expects sabab kategoriyalari (Material/Equipment/Operator/Tooling/Other) with visual coding and cost impact display. - _vizyon:_ COR-087 downtime = sabab plus vaqt plus responsibility. Reason enum with icon/color coding per category and cost impact visible. _/ build:_ reasonCodes dropdown from API endpoint. No visual coding per reason category, no cost or impact display, no categorization visible to operator.
  - **[P2]** Defect stage picker exists but no workflow linking to QC disposition. Vision COR-122 expects defect accountability: kirim braki versus shu bosqich braki differentiation. - _vizyon:_ Defect stage selection CRITICAL for accountability. Operator selects stage (kirim/sozdik/boyoq) - system auto-attributes responsibility. QC reclassifies if wrong. _/ build:_ Defect form has stage select dropdown, reportDefect mutation POSTs data. No stage legend/colors, no tooltip explaining stages, no responsibility attribution visible.

### To'g'rilik (ishlaydi != to'g'ri) - `PARTIAL` 42%
- **Vizyon kutadi:** **IoT Vizyon (Modul 16 — 50 savol, OCHIQ-JAVOBLAR + MODUL-SPEKTRI + AISHA-JARVIS):**

1. **Sensor telemetriyasi (kanonik):** Barcha mashinalarga sensorlar O'RNATILGAN (IoT-1 — hozir Excel/qo'lda). Sensordan IoT tabletka va Andon tabloga real-vaqt ma'lumot. IoT jadvallar (`iot_sensors`, `iot_sensor_readings`, `iot_alerts`, `sensor_devices`, `production_sessions`, `downtime_events`) to'liq DDL.

2. **Anomaliya aniqlash:** Sensor o'qilmasi chegaradan oshsa → avtomatik anomaliya yaratiladi (hajmi/bosim/temp/rg'i) + Telegram xabari (eskalatsiya matritsasi: smena-bosh→15m→sex-bosh→30m→direktor).

3. **MES ulanish:** IoT → MES sessiyasi (production_sessions `START/STOP/DEFECT` tabletdan). OEE hisoblash (Mavjudlik/Samaradorlik/Sifat). "iş yük"(material/reja kamchiligi) va sensor xatosi chiqarib tashlanadi (HR/RD tasdiqi bilan).

4. **QC/WMS integratsiya:** Brak sababini operator tabletda → MES/QC/HR hisoblariga yozilib, sifat nazorati harakatlari boshlanydi.

5. **AI kamera (markaziy):** Davomat (xudud-kirish + ish-joyiga vaqti), xavfsizlik (himoya vositasi yo'q, ziddiyat), xona inspeksiyasi (2-soat, ideal rasm). Jonli kamera hadisalari → `camera_events` jadvalga INSERT. Kamera hodisalari → inson tasdig'i → uyarmalar/bildirgi.

6. **Andon tablo:** Jonli (1-5m) barcha mashina qizil/sariq/yashil holati. Qo'xtosh, downtime eskalatsiya, smena KPI.

7. **Operator IoT tabletda TB-xavfsizlik chek-list** (IoT-079): Operator tasdiqlaydi lekin kamera "himoyasiz" aniqlamasa → ziddiyat log + audit. Kamera real-vaqt xavf (tigel yonida) → avto-signal darhol, keyin inson tasdig'i.

8. **Rejalashtirilgan profilaktika (PM) & Qolip almashtirish:** PM jadval texnik-kartada → ombor spare-qismi yo'qsa → avto-PR + tasdiq (texnik/sex boss).

9. **Energiya tannarxi:** Mashina quvvati × ish soati → GL harakat (tannarx). Qo'p mashina buyurtmada → taqsim (teng yoki ish-soati proportsioneli).

10. **Darslik/ЦКП biriktirilgan:** Kartaga darslik yo'q → oylik gate (E-ORG-111, E-HR-042, E-ORG-015). ЦКП to'liq kaskad (quyi→yuqori).

**MAVJUD (✅ jonli schema + handler + repository):**

1. **IoT Sensor Schema (`iot_sensors`, `iot_sensor_readings`, `iot_alerts`):** 
   - `iot_sensors`: id, sensor_code, name, type, machine_id, location, min_threshold, max_threshold, is_active, last_reading, last_reading_at
   - `iot_sensor_readings`: id, sensor_id, value, status, recorded_at (numeric value!)
   - `iot_alerts`: id, sensor_id, alert_type, severity, message, value, threshold, is_resolved, resolved_at
   - **Tayyor:** Batafsil, complete FK, check constraints (alert_type IN 'above_max','below_min','offline','error'; severity IN 'low','medium','high','critical')
   - **DB INSERT:** DrizzleSensorRepo.registerDevice() → INSERT iot_sensors (sensor_code, name, type, location...) → SQL.RETURNING

2. **Anomaliya Aniqlash (`AnomalyDetectedHandler`):**
   - File: `iot/infrastructure/event-handlers/anomaly-detected.handler.ts`
   - Method: `handle(AnomalyDetectedEvent)` → if threshold breach → emit Telegram bildirishov
   - **DB:** iot_alerts INSERT + isResolved=false + severity dynamic
   - **Status:** ✅ Handler registered in IotModule (event-handlers)

3. **Camera Schema + Events (`cameras`, `camera_events`, `camera_detections`, `safety_violations`, `camera_quality_defects`):**
   - `camera_events`: id, camera_id, user_id, zone_id, event_type ('telefon_ishlatish','uyqu','himoya_kiyimi_yoq','unknown_face' va h.k.), severity, status, screenshot_url, created_at
   - `camera_detections`: id, camera_id, user_id, detection_date/time, confidence, snapshot_url
   - `safety_violations`: id, camera_event_id, camera_id, user_id, violation_type, location, image_url
   - **Tayyor:** Strukturali, FK, aniqlangan event_type enums, confidence (0-1), imzo QC

4. **Production Session + OEE (`production_sessions`, `sensor_readings`, `downtime_events`):**
   - `production_sessions`: id, session_number, production_order_id, equipment_id, device_id (sensor), worker_id, status, target_qty, actual_qty, defect_qty, started_at, ended_at, OEE (availability, performance, quality, oee numeric)
   - `downtime_events`: id, session_id, event_type, started_at, ended_at, duration_seconds, reason_code ('material_shortage','tool_change','breakdown'...), reason_description
   - `sensor_readings`: id, device_id, pulse_count, reading_time, session_id, is_processed
   - **Tayyor:** Batafsil, session → device → sensor yog'lanishiga tayyor

5. **IoT Tablet endpoints (14 real CRUD):**
   - `POST /iot/tablet/sessions` → production_sessions yaratish (TabletSessionSchema parse)
   - `POST /iot/tablet/sessions/:id/start` → started_at, running_time
   - `POST /iot/tablet/sessions/:id/stop` → ended_at, calculate OEE
   - `POST /iot/tablet/sessions/:id/defect` → defect_qty + reason
   - `POST /iot/tablet/handover` → smena topshirish (shift_handover logic)
   - `POST /downtime-events` → reason_code + duration
   - **Status:** ✅ Wired to IotTabletService + DB

   - **Query:** SELECT malumoti (alerts, machines, OEE trends, attendance live, room inspections)

### Oqim / Integratsiya - `PARTIAL` 58%
- **Vizyon kutadi:** **Owner's Vision (from OCHIQ-JAVOBLAR EP-DIR-001..085):** Director dashboard = **5 KPIs unified (cash-flow + production-plan% + orders-in-pipeline + employees + quality)** with **5 holat levels** (GROWTH/NORMAL/CAUTION/RISK/CRISIS, color-coded). Real-time + daily-snapshot modes. **Data source:** Single canonical chain — Sales Orders → Planning → MES → QC → Warehouse → Finance GL (golden thread). **Automated:** KPI calculations from org-chart cards (card→GSD→oylik→razryad→portrait-match). **Financial reports:** Daily cron (18:00 Tashkent) snapshots 6 metrics (cash, warehouse, receivables, payables, balance sheet, production) from live module tables into rpt_* tables; alerts auto-sent Telegram. **Integration principle:** rpt_* = read-only aggregation points; no module data duplication; all reports trace back to single source (e.g., GL entries, warehouse_stock, production_fact, customer_payments). **Card-org-linked:** dashboard widgets auto-populate from card-ownership (karta→rahbar→widget visibility/threshold). **No golden-thread breaks allowed:** every KPI = traced lineage from transaction to card to person to dashboard to alert.
- **Qurilgan:** **LIVE BUILD VERIFIED:** ✅ **Reporting tables exist + cron jobs running:** 6 snapshot tables (rpt_kassa_transactions, rpt_ombor_qoldiq, rpt_debitorlar, rpt_kreditorlar, rpt_balans, rpt_ishlab_chiqarish) created in Drizzle schema (lib/db/src/schema/fi-financial-reports.ts:16-131). Financial cron registered (apps/api/src/modules/finance/financial-reports/cron/financial-reports-daily.cron.ts @Cron 18:00 Tashkent). ✅ **Cron → snapshot flow live:** Daily cron calls query service → fetches 6 metrics Promise.all (cash, warehouse, receivables, payables, balance, production) → persists via FinancialReportsRepository.saveKassa/saveOmbor/saveDebitors/saveCreditors/saveBalans/saveIshlab using db.insert(...).onConflictDoNothing() (idempotent). ✅ **Queries pull from source modules:** financial-reports-query.helpers.ts queries are REAL (not stubbed): queryCashSummary reads cashTransactions table (by date + transactionType) → aggregates inflow/outflow; queryWarehouseBalance reads warehouseTransactions (30-day avg); queryReceivables reads customer_payments (aging); queryPayables reads invoicePayments; queryBalanceSheet reads accounts + entries (GL); queryProductionMetrics reads production_fact table (raw SQL allowed per ARCHITECTURE comment). All return Result<T> pattern (no soxta). ✅ **Director dashboard integrated:** DirectorDashboard.tsx queries 10 endpoints (dash/production/finance/alerts/ai/kpi/validation/councils) + 5 MetricCard widgets auto-populate from prod/hr/fin/dash queries. Refetch intervals 30-120s (real-time). ✅ **Controller endpoints present:** dashboard.controller.ts GET /director/dashboard (P30 mode snapshot/realtime), GET /director/{plan-fact, order-progress, stat-trends, open-issues, production-summary, finance-summary, hr-summary} all delegate to DashboardQueryService → statRepo methods returning Row[] (safe Array.isArray checks). ✅ **Data aggregation service:** DashboardQueryService.getProductionSummary/getFinanceSummary/getHrSummary orchestrate Promise.all (3 queries each), unwrap Result<T>, fallback to 0/empty if error (degrade gracefully, dashboard never crashes). ✅ **Event publishing for approvals:** Director module publishes ApprovalRequestedEvent/ApprovedEvent/RejectedEvent (domain/events/) on approval state changes — aligns with HITL (Human-In-The-Loop) principle.
- **Chetlashishlar:**
  - **[P1]** Card→Dashboard linkage missing: Director dashboard shows global KPIs (5 holat, cash flow, production%, orders, HR, quality) but does NOT link to org_departments.head_user_id (card-ownership). Thresholds are hardcoded (e.g., (prod?.delayedOrders ?? 0) > 5 ? 'red') or in config tables (kpi_definitions) but NOT auto-derived from card's GSD + razryad. Per vision EP-DIR-001/071: KPI weights + thresholds = card-owned + org-hierarchy-aware. Hozir: global ERP-wide only, no dept-level drill-down tied to karta. - _vizyon:_ Each of 5 KPIs = card-sourced (karta.gsd_target + karta.weight + karta.razryad level). Dashboard thresholds = organizationally hierarchical (CEO see all; director see 7-dept; dept-head see own dept + sub-units) from org_departments tree. Holat level auto-computed per card-tree node. _/ build:_ DirectorDashboard.tsx has hard widget values (prod?.delayedOrders, hr?.absentToday, fin?.revenueVsLastMonth) fetched from global services. No card-ID filtering, no org-tree recursion, no dept-specific threshold override. kpi_definitions table exists (schema/director) but PATCH /director/kpi-definitions/:id (controller L155) is manual admin config, not org-sxema-aware.
  - **[P1]** Golden-thread break: Dashboard widgets disconnected from org-approval-workflow. Vision: Holat changes (NORMAL→CAUTION) should auto-trigger escalation via director.workflow_rules (Koordinatsiya-modul). Hozir: alerts sent Telegram (telegram.sendReport) but no structured approval-request event fired. No link from financial-alert (e.g., overdue debt > threshold) → create ApprovalRequest in director_approvals table. - _vizyon:_ Financial alerts (queryOverdueDebtAlerts, queryOverstockAlerts) → auto-emit events (e.g., 'OverdueDebtAlertEvent') → director.workflow_rules determines escalation path (owner karta → manager karta → director) → creates approval-request via HitlApprovalRequestedEvent → dashboard holat updates on approval state. _/ build:_ FinancialReportsDailyCron._checkAndSendAlerts (L85-116) sends Telegram to hardcoded groups ('kunlik'/'muammo'/'vazifa') but does NOT emit any domain event. Approval-request creation requires manual API call to POST /api/director/approvals (requires controller). No auto-escalation path from metric-breach → approval workflow.
  - **[P1]** Incomplete module interconnection: Production metrics query (queryProductionMetrics L162) reads production_fact table but does NOT aggregate from MES module sessions / OEE. Warehouse metrics read warehouseTransactions but NOT linked to WMS warehouse_stock canonical table. Receivables hardcoded 30% split (L98) rather than aging-bucket algorithm. - _vizyon:_ queryProductionMetrics = real OEE per production_sessions table (sm status=completed, calc efficiency=(good_qty/planned_qty)*100); queryWarehouseBalance = real warehouse_stock aggregation with FIFO/FEFO cost method; queryReceivables = proper aging (0-30/31-60/61-90/90+) bucketing per customer_payments.payment_date, not split-rate guess. _/ build:_ L162-194: raw SQL SELECT...FROM production_fact (correct table, but no OEE formula confirmation). L53-87: warehouseTransactions query OK (totalQuantity, 30d-avg) but no link to warehouse_stock.cost_method or warehouse_batches.fifo_rank. L89-109: receivables 30% hardcoded (Line 98: const overdue = ... total * 0.3) — wrong algorithm.
  - **[P2]** Stat-trends + open-issues endpoints not fully defined. Dashboard.tsx L60-62 calls getStatTrends() / getOpenIssues() but DashboardQueryService returns Row[] (empty fallback). Query repository implementations not found in grep (likely exist but semantics unclear). - _vizyon:_ stat-trends = daily stat-regulation (rpt-table?) progress toward OKR (org→department→card). open-issues = diary entries (issues.status != 'closed') with priority/assigned-karta. Both = real SQL from canonical tables. _/ build:_ DashboardQueryService.getStatTrends/getOpenIssues (L171-179) delegate to statRepo (concrete DashboardQueryRepository), which likely calls infrastructure/repositories/dashboard-query.repository.ts but NOT verified in search. Return type Row[] with safe Array.isArray fallback (defensive code). Implementation exists but semantics unverified.
  - **[P2]** Director state machine holat logic not exposed to frontend. Vision: holat = OSISH/NORMAL/EHTIYOT/XAVF/INQIROZ (color-coded), computed from 5-KPI threshold matrix. Hozir: frontend shows hardcoded colors (tone='red'/'amber'/'green') per metric, not unified holat enum. - _vizyon:_ POST /api/director/holat/:level with director-holat.constants.ts enum (GROWTH/NORMAL/CAUTION/RISK/CRISIS). Dashboard renders CompanyStateWidget with holat icon/color/narrative from director-state.service. _/ build:_ director-holat.service.ts + director-holat.constants.ts exist (apps/api/src/modules/director/application/). Enum likely defined. Frontend CompanyStateWidget.tsx (imported DirectorDashboard L187) renders state-widget but GET /director/holat endpoint not found in dashboard.controller.ts — likely missing controller method OR routed elsewhere (compatibility module?).
  - **[P2]** KPI weights not integrated with card-org-hierarchy. Dashboard L139-146 KpiSummaryCards displays KPI values but thresholds come from global config (kpi_definitions table with one-size-fits-all weights). Per vision EP-DIR-071: each card's weight = card-specific (card.kpi_weight_attendance/performance/tasks) + org-hierarchy-aggregated (roll-up child→parent). - _vizyon:_ Card-level weights in org_departments table (weight_attendance, weight_performance, weight_tasks columns). Dashboard KPI = weighted sum of child-department KPIs (recursive roll-up). Thresholds per department (some 80% is critical for one dept, 70% for another). _/ build:_ kpi_score_weights table (rpt layer, schema/director or compat?) with code/label/weight. PATCH /director/kpi-weights/:code updates global weights (L194-211). No per-card/per-department override. No rollup logic in DashboardQueryService (each method returns flat Result<T>, not tree).
