### UI / UX - `PARTIAL` 62%
- **Vizyon kutadi:** VISION (from digest — owner's 20-module spec): 
**Hisobot direktordagi holat = 5 ko'rsatkich (pul oqimi, i.ch reja%, buyurtma, xodim, sifat; vazn sozlanadi) + 5 holat darajasi (O'SISH/NORMAL/EHTIYOT/XAVF/INQIROZ, rangli) + kechikshi majburiy sabab + real-time dashboard (modullardan avto) + kunlik AI strategik digest + egaga 5 raqam (Yangi/Yo'qolgan/Kichik mijoz + Savdo trendi + Eng katta xavf) + Telegram digest + OKR (kompaniya→bo'lim→karta).**

Xarita-Reja doc says: "kompaniya holati = 5 ko'rsatkich birga (pul oqimi+i.ch reja%+buyurtma+xodim+sifat, sozlanadigan vazn); 5 holat darajasi (O'SISH/NORMAL/EHTIYOT/XAVF/INQIROZ, rangli); kechikshi majburiy sabab kategoriyasi (root-cause); real-time dashboard (modullardan avto-to'ladi); kunlik strategik AI; egaga 5 raqam (Yangi/Yo'qolgan/Kichiklashayotgan mijoz + Savdo trendi + Eng katta xavf); Telegram digest; OKR."

Ключевые маркеры: (1) 5 КПИ взвешенных агрегированных (не все 20 метрик на одной странице) (2) 5 состояний = цветной статус компании (3) конкретные 5 чисел для владельца (4) AI ежедневный дайджест (5) OKR иерархия (6) Telegram уведомления.
- **Qurilgan:** JONLI KOD:
BE (DirectorDashboard.controller.ts:52-76): 
- ✅ GET /director/dashboard → 9 parallel query (users, orders, approvals, planFact, orderProgress, statTrends, openIssues, production-summary, finance-summary) + mode=snapshot|realtime (P30: 07:00 frozen vs live)
- ✅ GET /director/kpis → KPI definitions + values (soglanuvchi thresholds)
- Но KPI_WEIGHT табли заполнена (kpi_score_weights ✅ 4 строки)
- ✅ CompanyStateWidget FE → 5 состояний (osish/normal/ehtiyot/xavf/inqiroz) + 4 метрики (profit%, revenue%, retention%, history)
- ✅ 5 главных чисел для владельца: delayed orders, 24h SLA overdue (inbox), critical stock, absent today, defect% 
- ⚠️ "5 koʻrsatkich" — только 4 показаны: profit_pct/revenue_pct/retention_pct/history; **покупатели (Yangi/Yo'qolgan/Savdo trendi) НЕТ** (вижу SalesSummaryCard но без 5 специфичных цифр)
- ⚠️ Kunlik AI strategik digest: AIAdvisor.tsx + AISummaryCard.tsx есть, но Q ЕСТЬ ли REAL AI (gemini/claude) yoki STUB (hardcoded)? → no LLM call in code, likely stub
- ❌ OKR компания→bo'lim→karta иерархия: НЕТУ (только dashboard KPI, no cascading)
- ❌ Telegram digest: НЕТУ (API есть но no bot integration to send daily digest)

FE (DirectorDashboard.tsx):
- ✅ Respons grid (1/2/3 col layout)
- ✅ Real-time refetch (60-120sec interval)
- ✅ Loading states (useQuery isLoading)
- ✅ Widget-based layout (CompanyStateWidget, AIAdvisor, AlertFeed, ModuleHealthGrid, KpiSummaryCards, 3-column card grid)

- ❌ 5 чисел для владельца (Yangi/Yo'qolgan/Savdo trendi) — НЕ видно как отдельный widget или section; SalesSummaryCard существует (дневная продажа) но не "5 спец цифр"

ИТОГ: Dashboard UI читаема и функциональна (KPI, state, alerts, production, HR, finance, warehouse в 3-колонке), но НЕ ПОЛНОСТЬЮ соответствует 5 ключевым требованиям от хозяина.
- **Chetlashishlar:**
  - **[P1]** 5 ключевых цифр для собственника (Yangi/Yo'qolgan/Kichiklashayotgan mijoz + Savdo trendi + Eng katta xavf) отсутствуют как выделенный виджет/раздел - _vizyon:_ Диджест для владельца: (1) Новые клиенты (неделя), (2) Потерянные клиенты (неделя), (3) Сокращающиеся клиенты, (4) Тренд продаж, (5) Самый большой риск компании — все в одном месте (dashboard верхняя часть или sidebar) _/ build:_ SalesSummaryCard(dirSum) показывает текущие заказы (+/-), но НЕ имеет специфичных '5 чисел'. MetricCard(5 штук) показывает: delayed orders, 24h inbox overdue, critical stock, absent, defect% — это не то же самое. Нет выделенного 'Owner 5-Point Summary' виджета.
  - **[P1]** Kunlik strategik AI digest — непонятно real-time summary vs ежедневный отчет; нет автоматизированного отправки Telegram/Email - _vizyon:_ Каждый день в 07:00 (или konf время): AI генерирует стратегический дайджест (ключевые риски, достижения, рекомендации) → отправляет владельцу в Telegram (или email). Дайджест видим на dashboard ('kunlik strategik AI'). _/ build:_ AIAdvisor & AISummaryCard есть (реагируют на live /api/director/ai-summary), но: (a) no cron/scheduler для 'kunlik' (ежедневный); (b) /api/.../ai-summary возвращает stub-summary (no real LLM call видно); (c) no Telegram/Email integration в коде (API route есть, но no sender). Это real-time widget, не ежедневный digest.
  - **[P2]** OKR иерархия (kompaniya→bo'lim→karta) отсутствует в dashboard - _vizyon:_ Dashboard должен показывать: Company-level OKR (5 КПИ) → Department level (7 отделений) → Card level (каждая должность своя ЦКП). Cascade view (drill-down). _/ build:_ KpiSummaryCards & KpiScorecard показывают flat KPI list (не иерархия). ModuleHealthGrid показывает 20 модулей здоровье, но не OKR. Нет drill-down компания→отдел→карта. Это NOT BUILT.
  - **[P2]** ЦКП (GSD) kunlik hisobot deadline (16 soat yoki 3 soat) — не видно в dashboard как отдельная метрика/alert - _vizyon:_ Dashboard: для каждого отделения показать процент xodim'ов которые сдали ЦКП (GSD daily report) вовремя (до 16:00 или 3 часов deadline). Red alert если не сдали. _/ build:_ HRStatusCard показывает attendance, но НЕ показывает GSD report submission rate. Нет widget'а для этого метрика.
  - **[P2]** Kechikshi (overdue) sabab kategoriyasi (root-cause) — dashboard показывает что есть kechikshi но НЕ почему (kategoriya) - _vizyon:_ Overdue orders card должен показать: '5 kechikshi | Sabab: Material (2), Setup (1), QC (1), Operator (1)' _/ build:_ OverdueOrdersCard показывает массив overdue orders (id, papkaNo, tayyorBolishSanasi) но NO root-cause categorization (Material/Setup/QC/Machine/Operator). Array items читаемы но не aggregated by cause.

**Direkto dashboard (EP-DIR-001 .. EP-DIR-033):** Kompaniya holati = 5 ko'rsatkich birga (pul oqimi + ishlab chiqarish reja% + buyurtma + xodim + sifat), har biriga **sozlanadigan vazn** (kpi_definitions jadval). 5 daraja holat (OSISH/NORMAL/EHTIYOT/XAVF/INQIROZ, rangli). Kechikish = majburiy sabab kategoriyasi (material/transport/operator/qolip/boshqa) → root-cause tahlil. Karta produkt = **moslashuvchan 2-4** (lavozimga qarab).

**KPI tizimi (kpi_definitions / kpi_score_weights):** 
- kpi_definitions: KPI kodi + nomi + kategoriya + birlik + target_value + warning_threshold + critical_threshold → direktor sozlaydi
- kpi_score_weights: HR KPI vazni (davomat/faollik/vazifa) → direktor sozlaydi

**Kunlik hisobot (daily-report):**
- Kunlik ЦКП kiritiladi/keladi → agregat → deadline o'tmasa kun-oyligi yoziladi
- AI-chatbot kunlik savol (mashinasiz, AI-gated)
- IoT/MES → karta avto-feed
- Mashinachi IoT qiymat, mashinasiz xodim AI-chatbot orqali
- Kunlik hisobot deadline (spec: 16 soat vs BARCHA_JAVOBLAR 3 soat — **ZIDDIYAT, egasi yakuniy raqam beradi**)
- Hisobot deadline o'tmasa kun-oyligi yoziladi (oylik-gate)

**Real-time dashboard:**
- Avto-to'ldirish / Excel ustunlari: reja%, counters, downtime, ish-joyiga kelish, energiya, davomat, reja/fakt/qoldiq, brak, smena, ishchi-norma%, operatsiya-norma, lak, tayyorlik%, sikl-vaqt, priladka, trend, snapshot

**AI tahlil va tavsiya:**
- Kunlik strategik AI (kompaniya holati tahlili)
- Kichik-buyurtma tahlil
- Razmer-optim tavsiya
- Trend→holat avto-qabul

- Aralashish-riski alert

### Oqim / Integratsiya - `PARTIAL` 62%
- **Vizyon kutadi:** MES = oltin-ip yadrosi. PP->MES->QC->WMS->FIN uzluksiz zanjir. MES->QC event-driven (MesCompletedEvent). Operator IoT-tablet: material norma avto-hisob + operator tasdig'i. 3-bosqich sessiya (sozlash/asosiy/yakunlash) OEE aniq. Jonli sex-tablo 1-5 daq. Downtime bosqichli eskalatsiya. Bonus ball A/B/C->taklif->HR tasdiq (global adolat). Karta-integratsiya: operator razryad + GSD kaskad. Darslik-gate: LMS sertifikat hard block. 8-status state machine.
- **Qurilgan:** Kod qurilgan: Production sessions (pp-iot.ts 8-status); ProductionSession aggregate; start/complete handlers CQRS; LMS sertifikat tekshiruvi (optional gate); MesCompletedEvent->QC listener (INSERT qc_inspections order_id=ppId reference_type=production_order); OEE columns exist; downtime aggregate+events+handlers; MesToHr360Event (Trigger 16). DB: work_centers (certificationLmsCourseId, requiredSkillName, normaM2/KgPerShift). Repository queries production_sessions table (Drizzle). Event listeners (4 LMS-related). Session start binds operatorId, workCenterId, productionOrderId.

### UI / UX - `PARTIAL` 32%
- **Vizyon kutadi:** Vision (EP-MES-001..082 + docs): Operator tablet interface displays A смена План (shift plan) form showing: (1) 3-phase session (tayyorgarlik→sozlash→asosiy→yakunlash); (2) Mashina/operator/yordamchi joylashuvi; (3) Real-time reja-fakt taqqoslash; (4) Jonli bandlik (kim qaysi mashinada); (5) Norma + actual miqdor; (6) Downtime + sabab; (7) OEE, unum, defect; (8) Smena yakuni (handover tasdiq); (9) Non-blocking checklists with TB xavfsizlik; (10) 3 shift (A/B/C) with doimiy brigadalar. Status = 3 bosqich sessiyasi (ready→checklist_pending→running→paused→completed→sent_to_qc). Operator kiritishes ketma-ket (off-line sync later), tasdiq majburiy pasyalar uchun. UI izchil EP design tokens (shablon ListPage/DetailPage/FormPage emas, faqat custom tabs + oee/reasons/maintenance/sos/norms). Tab ≤2 daraja majburiy. Forma REAL saqlaydi (FE mutation→BE→DB). Mashinasiz xodimlar kunlik GSD hisoboti AI-chatbot orqali qo'lda kiritiladi (IoT yo'q).
- **Qurilgan:** Hozir qurilgan (jonli kod + DB): (1) **Backend:** MES module bor — `MesProductionSessionsController` (GET list, POST create, GET :id, POST :id/downtime); `ProductionSession` aggregate (6 status: ready/checklist_pending/running/paused/completed/sent_to_qc); `MesProductionSessionsService` (CRUD); repository pattern; Result<T> pattern majburiy; Drizzle ORM `mesProductionSessions` jadval (orderId/operatorId/machineId/shiftId/sessionDate/status/producedQty/defectQty/startTime/endTime). (2) **FE:** MESExtended.tsx route (5 tab: OEE/Reasons/Maintenance/Norms/Sos) — hali CUSTOM TABS emas (ListPage/DetailPage/FormPage shablon ishlatilmaydi). MESDowntimes.tsx alohida; MESExtendedDialogs.tsx dialog komponentlar. Queries: /api/mes/oee, /mes/maintenance-requests, /mes/gamification/leaderboard, /mes/stats, /mes/downtime-reasons, /mes/shifts/current, /mes/tasks, /mes/sos/history, /pp/work-centers. (3) **Schema:** `production_sessions` (pp-iot.ts) — более FULL (ppId/workCenterId/operatorId/status/targetQuantity/actualQuantity/defectQuantity/startedAt/completedAt/downtimes). `mesProductionSessions` (mes-schema.ts) — simpler (orderId/operatorId/machineId/shiftId/sessionDate/status/producedQty/defectQty/startTime/endTime). TWO OVERLAPPING jadvallar hali tozalanmagan (ikki-olam). - **Vizyon kutadi:** QC modul vizyondan: (1) STANDARD AQL 2.5 – namuna hajmi partiya hajmiga qarab (ISO 2859-1); (2) DEFEKT 3 DARAJA (kritik 0% o'tmaydi, jiddiy, kichik) – severity ≠ sort; (3) SORT 1/2/3 + BRAK – har biriga narx koeffitsienti (yaroqli mahsulot tashlanmaydi, arzonroq sotiladi); (4) BRAK SABABI – "kirim braki" vs "shu bosqich braki" alohida (adolatli); (5) SERTIFIKAT – avto-PDF, raqam SF-2026-NNNNN, QR, laborant+boshliq imzo, uz/ru/en; (6) GOLDEN THREAD: MES.complete → Trigger 10 (QC PENDING ochish) → QC.pass → Trigger 11 (WMS FG receipt) → FIN GL entry; (7) EVENT PUBLIKATSIYA – QcPassedEvent, QcFailedEvent, SupplierQualityFailEvent; (8) OYLIK GATE – QC NATIJAI xodim ta'rifi (karta-asosli) bilan bog'lanadi; (9) DARSLIK GATE – QC va LMS integratsiyasi; (10) AI BAHO – kamera + defect-detector + FMEA (RPN, CRITICAL>200/HIGH>100/MEDIUM>50); (11) ATRIBUSIYA – oltin zanjirda: CRM lead → SD → PP → MES → QC → WMS → FIN, bitta haqiqat bilan.
- **Qurilgan:** JIVA QURILGAN: (1) AQL 2.5 ✅ – ISO 2859-1 to'liq jadvallar qodirlangan (qc-aql.constants.ts); (2) DEFEKT 3 DARAJA ✅ – severity enum (critical/major/minor); (3) BRAK SIFATI ✅ – qc_braks jadval (stage/quantity/reason/cost_impact); (4) SERTIFIKAT ✅ – qc_certificates jadval (certificate_number, issued_by, expires_at); (5) GOLDEN THREAD ✅ – MesCompletedListener (Trigger 10: qc_inspections open) → submit-inspection.handler (publish QcPassedEvent) → QcPassedListener (Trigger 11: ReceiveFgCommand → WMS) – REAL event-based; (6) EVENTS ✅ – QcPassedEvent, QcFailedEvent, SupplierQualityFailEvent qc/domain/events, registered in qc.module.ts; (7) FMEA + DPMO ✅ – RPN, SPC X-bar/R, DPMO hisob; (8) REPOSITORY ✅ – DrizzleQcInspectionRepository, defects.repo, reclamations.repo (DDD); (9) CONTROLLERS ✅ – 6 ta (inspections, defects, reclamations, extended, new, parameters); (10) 21 ta QC jadval ✅ – standards, parameters, material_tests, final_inspections, defects, braks, reclamations, certificates, root_causes, spc_data, checkpoints, lab_tests; (11) INTEGRATION POINT – WMS'da qc-passed.listener: QcPassedEvent → ReceiveFgCommand (orderId + materialId + qty). DB-PROOF: 1 qc_inspection (order 48, passed), 0 qc_defects, 1 qc_reclamation, 0 qc_braks. ### To'g'rilik (ishlaydi != to'g'ri) - `PARTIAL` 35%
- **Vizyon kutadi:** **Egasi vizyoni (EP-QC-003, -005, -072, -090):**
1. **AQL 2.5 standart** — ISO 2859-1 namunaviy nazorat (partiya hajmi→kod harfi→namuna soni+ac/re chegaralari). AQL 2.5 = standart.
2. **3 darajali defekt og'irligi** — CRITICAL (0% o'tmas) / MAJOR (jiddiy) / MINOR (kichik kosmetik, chegara bilan). Kritik defektda buyurtma to'xtaydi.
3. **EP-QC-072 YETISHMAYAPTISI:** Sort/grading = **1-sort / 2-sort / 3-sort / brak**, har biriga **narx koeffitsienti** (1.0 / 0.75 / 0.5 / 0.0). Yaroqli mahsulot (sort 1-3) arzonroq sotiladi, tashlanmaydi. `qc_inspections.sort_grade` + `qc_sort_price_config` jadvallar kerak. **Hozir YO'Q.**
4. **EP-QC-090 Brak sababchisi** — "kirim braki" (oldingi bosqich MM/WMS qobuliga kelib tushunga yoki PP/MES bosqichida) vs "shu bosqich braki" (QC o'ziga sodir) alohida kaydlanadi = adolatli mas'uliytni taqsimlab oladi. Hozir `qc_braks.reason` faqat sof sabab qatorlar (misprint/size/moisture/etc), sababning MANBAI (incoming vs production) qayd etilmaydi.
5. **Sertifikat (A-default):** SF-2026-NNNNN ketma-ket raqami, uz/ru/en shablon, QR, laborant+QC rahbar imzolari, real test natijalari bilan avto-PDF. Hozir `qc_certificates` VIEW mavjud (LMS to'qima), lekin QC-maxsus (defekt yoki parametr test natijalari) sertifikat EMAS.
- **Qurilgan:** **QURILGAN (JONLI TEKSHIRISH):**
- ✅ **AQL 2.5 konstanta** mavjud: `qc-aql.constants.ts` faylda ISO 2859-1 Table 1 va 2A (code letters, Ac/Re) to'liq kodlangan, AQL 2.5 = default (EP-QC-003 tasdiq). SERVICE'ga qo'llanish qabula oladi (hozir stub/integration test talabodan yuq).
- ✅ **3 darajali defekt og'irligi** kodlangan: `DefectSeverity` enum = CRITICAL/MAJOR/MINOR (3 ta), qc_defects jadvalida mavjud.
- ❌ **EP-QC-072 Sort/grading = BUZILGAN:** Jadvallar YETISHMAYAPTI. `qc_inspections.sort_grade` USTUNI YO'Q (faqat test natijalari: pass_count, fail_count, total_count). `qc_sort_price_config` jadval MAVJUD EMAS (docs say kerak, BUILD qilinmagan). **NATIJA:** Finished goods "sort 1-3/brak" aniqlastirilmaydi, har hamma mahsulot "normal" deb tasniflanadi; narx koeffitsienti qo'llanila olmaydi.
- ⚠️ **EP-QC-090 Brak sababchisi YETARLI EMAS:** `qc_braks.reason` enum faqat `'incoming'/'production'/'final'/'warehouse'` BOSQICHNI, lekin ILDIZ MANBAI (MM kirim vs PP bosqichi EMAS) qayd etilmaydi. Masalan: `stage='incoming'` o'ng, lekin "MM'dan yoki oldingi PP bosqichdan kelgan" farq YO'Q.
- ✅ **Sertifikat shabloni (A-default)** mavjud: LMS `certificates` VIEW (user_id, course_id, certificate_number), lekin QC-maxsus **product/order sertifikati (test natijalari bilan)** EMAS — faqat kurs sertifikati. Docs talaba qiladi: SF-2026-NNNNN format, uz/ru/en shablon, test natijalari (parameters), laborant+QC rahbar imzolari, QR. Hozir qc_certificates faqat view, insertion test natijalari yo'q.

**DB HOLAT (q.cjs):**

- qc_braks: 0 ta
- qc_defects: 0 ta
- qc_reclamations: 1 ta

WMS'da finished goods sort-bo'yicha saqlanadi. Invoiceda unit narx = sort koeffitsienti × base narx. Namuna olinadi, defektlar sanaladi. Defektlar soni: 0≤d≤Ac → ACCEPT, d≥Re → REJECT. AQL 2.5 (ISO 2859-1) standart. _/ build:_ qc-aql.constants.ts faylda AQL_LOT_SIZE_TABLE + SAMPLING_PLANS kodlangan, lekin qc_new.repository.ts yoki qc-parameters.service.ts'da `calculateAqlPlan(lotSize, level)` integratsiyasi EMAS. API /qc/get-aql yoki /qc/calculate-sample → test data EMAS.
  - **[P2]** CRITICAL defekt bloking logikasi (buyurtma to'xtishi) implementation YO'Q - _vizyon:_ Defektseverity=CRITICAL bo'lsa: buyurtmani to'xtatzish, QC approval kutish, xodim notification. Auto-stop TAQIQ. _/ build:_ DefectSeverity.CRITICAL enum mavjud, lekin qc-defects.controller.ts'da CRITICAL qayta ongahmaydi (report-defect handler → just save, no stop-production logic). ## 14. SD / sotuv-buyurtma - o'rtacha 49% (5 tahlil)

### Asosiy g'oya / Model - `PARTIAL` 58%
- **Vizyon kutadi:** Vizyon OCHIQ-JAVOBLAR 2026-06-08 (EP-SD-* savollari, 24 ochiq 138/138 hal): (1) KANONIK BUYURTMA = bitta `sales_orders` jadval, menejer kiritadi (SO-4500012345 formatida, documentNumber unique). (2) KANONIK MODEL = `sales_orders` → `sd_order_departments` fan-out (buyurtma 7-otdeleniyening har biriga vertikal yo'l — Vysotskiy-7 model, org_departments'ga bog'langan, manager_id zanjiri bilan hierarchical workflow). (3) TASDIQ MARSHRUTI = org-sxema bo'yicha vertikal (manager_id = bevosita rahbar) + gorizontal (workflow_rules, admin tuzatadi, hardcoded EMAS). (4) OLTIN IP = Material kutish ("Ожд.Сырьё") → Ta'minot'ga avto signal, sotuvga ko'rinadi.
- **Qurilgan:** JONLI KOD-DB TEKSHIRUVI: (1) `sales_orders` MAVJUD (13 satrli data, kanonik documentNumber), SAP-ustuli (salesOrg/distributionChannel). (2) STATUS FLOW = overallStatus + masterStatus zanjiri (27-state). (3) ADVANS = advancePercent(default 70) + advanceStatus — QURILGAN. (4) `sd_order_departments` SKELET (0 qator, department=VARCHAR, org_departments FK YO'Q). (5) WORKFLOW_RULES JADVAL = YO'Q. (6) OLTIN IP signal (Material→MM) = EVENT_KATALOGI mavjud, lekin SD-MM listener missing. (7) Biznes qarorlar (avans, jarima, tiraj) = HARDCODED seed, admin panel yo'q.

### To'liqlik - `PARTIAL` 38%
- **Vizyon kutadi:** SD = Golden Thread spine (oltin-ip yadrosi): menejer buyurtma ochadi → kanonik `sales_orders` → 70% avans gate → delivery → GL posting → invoice bilishing. Manager-centered (menejer buyurtma-paneli): kotirovka (КП) rasmiy hujjat, versiyalandi, PDF, imzo (EP-SD-003/004/111); advance 70% + 5-day payment terms (EP-SD-023/128/130: 100% avans → 5% auto-discount); order status janjira (7 bosqich: draft→advance_pending→advance_paid→...→delivered→closed); merchandise via `sd_order_departments` fan-out (7 FK masterlashed); customer master (24000 customers, A/B/C ABC rating, debitor tracking); quotations (kotirovka) versioning + PDF + manager signature gate (karta-model RBAC); Telegram/Whatsapp lead channels; invoice auto-generation; advance-bypass governance (rahbar tasdiq); delivery recording (EXTERNAL_OUT gate = ombor+moliya+AI); klishe/shtamp registry (ownership + archive period); sales KPI (weekly volume/closed deals/average deal size/debitor control) + leaderboard + menejer bonus → HR/Moliya tasdiq. SD = karta-model hub: menejer shukhvat ro'zi ojiga, rahbar tasdiq/chegirma, komdir КП imzo. Kanonik jadval = `sales_orders` (sales_order_items, sd_order_departments, sd_quotations, sd_payments, sd_invoices); `sd_sales_orders` = VIEW compatibility. Har ma'lumot real DB, soxta echo/fake/hardcoded TAQIQ.
- **Qurilgan:** Backend structure = SdModule (435 /api/sd/* routes) + DDD (aggregate SalesOrder, events OrderCreated/OrderStatusChanged, handlers). DB: `sales_orders` kanonik (1 qator seed, id=1), `sd_sales_orders` VIEW, `sd_customers=0`, `sd_order_departments=0`, `sd_quotations=0`, `sd_payments=0`, `sd_invoices=0`. Controllers: sd-orders, sd-quotations, sd-customers, sd-payments, sd-invoices, sd-deliveries, sd-leads, sd-contracts. Commands: create-order.handler (yazilgan lekin real order creation logic?), confirm-advance-payment.handler, approve-advance-bypass.handler, create-invoice.handler. Queries: list-orders, get-order-by-id, pending-advance-orders, get-invoices, atp-check. SalesOrder aggregate bor: orderNumber, status, advanceRequired=70, advancePaid, advanceStatus (pending|partial|approved|bypassed), designFlag, sampleFlag, techApprovals (BOM/Routing/Card). But real data = 0 customers, 0 orders, 0 quotations. FE: /sd/sales-orders (form/table), /sd/customers (360 panel), /sd/quotations (list), /sd/invoices (dashboard), /sd/deliveries, /sd/payments. (Live status: seed data yo'q, logic jadvallari mavjud, birinchi order CREATE ni tekshirish kerak.)

  - **[P0]** Menejer buyurtma ochasi tizimi yo'q: kanonik order CREATE yo'q / tuzatilmagan. - _vizyon:_ Menejer /sd/sales-orders da buyurtma (kotirovka→buyurtma) ochadi; kanonik `sales_orders` qatarida saqlandi; avans 70% talab qiladi (EP-SD-001/023/055). _/ build:_ SalesOrder aggregate + create-order.handler bor, lekin real INSERT test yo'q (DB 0 qator). ### Oqim / Integratsiya - `PARTIAL` 52%
- **Vizyon kutadi:** SD (Sales/Distribution) module = golden-thread yadrosi. Menejer buyurtma kiritadi (kanonik `sales_orders` jadval) → org-departmentga (karta) bog'lanadi + klishe/shtamp mijoz to'laydi → zavodda saqlanadi (~3 yil). Buyurtma bekor jarimasi bosqichli (maket 30%/bosildi 70%/tayyor 100%). Tirajdan og'ish ±10%. Kredit limit (oshsa blok→direktor tasdiq). Kotirovka 14 kun. "Ожд.Syrьё"→Ta'minotga avto signal. Buyurtma manbai kuzatiladi. OLTIN IP: SD→PP→MES→QC→WMS→FIN — bitta uzluksiz zanjir, real DB-proof; bitta kanonik jadval (sales_orders). OrderCreatedEvent → prod-planning (PP) avtomatik rejalashtiradi → MES ishlab chiqarish → QC sifat → WMS ombor → FIN (GL entries). Hamma bosqich real INSERT va kuzatiladigan natija (echo/fake taqiq).
- **Qurilgan:** DB jonli: sales_orders=13 qator, sales_invoices=0, sales_order_items=2; production_orders FK sales_order=1 qator; deliveries=1, qc_inspections=1, entries=6 (GL). Schema: sales_orders 75 ustun (order_number, status, total_amount, customer_id, assigned_to (UUID), created_by (UUID), org_department_id topilmadi, ammo assigned_to/created_by bor). OrderCreatedEvent domain event kviyadi (kompakt: orderId, companyId, orderNumber, totalAmount). Listeners: deal-won (SD), order-created-delivery (Logistics), order-created-kanban (Kanban), order-created-notification (Notifications), sales-order-ready-planning (PP). Handler: CreateOrderHandler (DDD, Result pattern, aggregate). Kod quality: E2E testlar mavjud (sd-orders.controller.e2e-spec.ts, sd-invoices, sd-quotations). Integration: outbox + event-bus, handler quyi-layer.
- **Chetlashishlar:**
  - **[P1]** Kanonik jadval = sales_orders lekin org_department_id FK YO'Q (karta bog'lanishi yo'q). assigned_to/created_by UUID-dan, org_departments (144 qator) kartalardan to'g'ridan FK yo'q. Vizyon: 'Menejer buyurtma kiritadi → org-departmentga bog'lanadi' — kupalak: org-structure.module'da team/department yo'q SD'da, sales_orders eski user_id pointer ishlatadi. - _vizyon:_ sales_orders.org_department_id → org_departments.id FK (kanonik). Buyurtma har karta/bo'limga bog'lanadi. Menejer = karta (assigned_to SHOULD be to karta_id, not UUID user). Karta ro'yxatidagi 'Buyurtmalar' tab menejerga bog'langan buyurtmalarni ko'rsatadi. _/ build:_ sales_orders.assigned_to = varchar UUID (users.id yo'qsa bo'sh). org_department_id ustuni MAVJUD emas. sales_orders schema 75 ustun — kupalak: org-department bog'lanishi database-level yo'q (faqat application-level talaba).
  - **[P1]** Golden thread 6 bosqich (SD→PP→MES→QC→WMS→FIN) jonli oqim test'da faqat 1 production_order + 1 delivery + 1 qc_inspection bo'yicha 1 sales_order'da bog'lanadi. Qolgan 12 sales_order → PP/MES/QC/WMS/FIN'ga OQIM'yi tuzatilmadi (12 orphan). - _vizyon:_ Har sales_order: 1. OrderCreatedEvent → 2. PP (production_orders qatori+planning) → 3. (6) **GL Avtomatik** — to'lov tasdig'i → GL kirim yozuvi, debi-kredi avto-hisob. (7) **Karta-model RBAC** — menejer o'z buyurtmalari, rahbar bo'limi, direktor hammasi; tannarx/margin RBAC-qo'shimcha (rahbar+). (8) **Oltin-ip Integrity** — sales_orders.id = PP→MES→QC→WMS→GL barchada FK (bitta raqam), yagona haqiqat, tarix immutable. (9) **Buyurtma Statusi Real** — Производство.xlsx: В процессе/Ожд.Сырьё/Ожд.Производство/Готов/Завершен/Отменен (egasi real talab); zavodda bajarilgan qilishi shunga qarab master_status shu 6 yoki 24-state ekivalenti qo'yilishi kerak.

## 15. CRM - o'rtacha 50% (5 tahlil)

### Asosiy g'oya / Model - `PARTIAL` 45%
- **Vizyon kutadi:** Voronka (funnel)-based CRM with multi-entity lead/deal/contact management (Bitrix-style). Leads (potensial mijozlar) → conversions → Deals + linked Contacts + Activities. CRITICAL: CRM integrates into **KARTA-markazli** (card-centric) organizational model — every employee/role is a KARTA in org_departments; CRM MUST reference kartalar (cards), not hardcoded manager_id integers. Sales manager (savdo menejer) is a KARTA; their interactions, contacts, deals are KARTA-owned (via assignedById → users.id → org_cards.user_id). Ownership chain: Interaction → Sales Manager KARTA → Org hierarchy (Vysotskiy 7 levels). ALL Q1-Q55 questions assume this KARTA-centric model: corporate phone "біркitted kartasiga", task routing "bo'lim kartasi orqali", permission checks "karta RBAC'dan". CRM model lacks **explicit card-entity mapping** — no crm_leads.card_id, crm_deals.card_id, crm_contacts.card_id FK; rely on assignedById→users→card deduction (inferred, fragile).
- **Qurilgan:** CRM schema (lib/db/src/schema/crm-contacts.ts, crm-pipelines.ts) uses BITRIX24 data model: **crm_leads** (id, title, sourceId, statusId, assignedById, phones[], emails[]), **crm_deals** (id, title, stageId, companyId, assignedById, opportunity), **crm_contacts** (id, name, companyId, assignedById), **customerInteractions** (type: call/email/meeting, customerId, managerId, interactionDate). Manager/Owner = **assignedById (integer) → users.id** (not direct card reference). Org hierarchy **departments** table (parentId tree, managerId) is SEPARATE from CRM — no FK/bridge table linking crm_* to departments. Schema analysis: crm_leads.assignedById references users(id); users table (core-schema.ts) has **no explicit card_id or department_id**. Thus: CRM->users->??? chain breaks; cannot deduce which KARTA owns the lead/deal without a **users.card_id column** (missing) or **crm_leads.card_id FK** (missing). Consequence: (A) RBAC/permission checks on CRM data must infer card from user context (not schema-enforced); (B) Interaction routing to "Daromadlar bo'limi KARTA" (Q6) impossible without explicit routing rules (not present); (C) "Menejer ketganda kartasiga bog'lanmagan qarz" (Q33 HR aging) — CRM has no mechanism to detect card ownership change.
- **Chetlashishlar:**
  - **[P1]** Missing card_id foreign key in CRM entities - _vizyon:_ All CRM entities (crm_leads, crm_deals, crm_contacts, customer_interactions) MUST have card_id FK → org_cards/departments, making KARTA-ownership explicit and enforceable in DB schema. Ownership chain: lead → card → user→card hierarchy → Org-sxema. Enables: (1) permission checks `lead.card_id == user.card_id`, (2) org-chart routing (Q6 qarzdorlik → Daromadlar bo'limi card), (3) card-lifecycle impact (card vakant → redistribute leads), (4) ЦКП aggregation (team sales KPI by card). _/ build:_ crm_leads, crm_deals, crm_contacts, customer_interactions all have assignedById (→users.id) but NO card_id FK. users table has no card_id column. CRM ownership inferred, not enforced. No bridge/junction table linking CRM to org_departments. Consequence: schema cannot enforce 'only your card's leads' RBAC; routing by org-chart impossible; card lifecycle (takeover, vacation) does not auto-update CRM ownership.
  - **[P1]** CRM does not reference unified corporate-number (korporativ raqam) model - _vizyon:_ Q1-Q5 vision: Corporate phone is tied to card (not user). When new savdo menejer (card) is created, corporate raqam assigned to card; when card holder leaves, raqam auto-transfers to next card-holder in same role. Contact history preserved under card, not user. CRM musttrack interactions via card+raqam, not user+phone. _/ build:_ customerInteractions has managerId (integer) + no corporate-phone model. crm_leads/contacts have phones[] (JSONB array). No 'corporate_phone' table linking card→raqam. No lifecycle (on card-vacant, transfer raqam+history to new holder).
  - **[P2]** Qarzdorlik (Q6-Q8) routing and RBAC not modeled - _vizyon:_ CRM should route debt-collection tasks to Daromadlar bo'limi (Revenue Dept) KARTA, not sales manager. Schema: (1) customer debt linked to card that made sale, (2) task creation 'qarz undirish' routed to Revenue Dept card (org-chart lookup), (3) permission matrix: savdo-menejer READ-only qarz status (Finance → Daromadlar updates; savdo cannot manipulate). crm_customers.debt_status WRITE permission matrix by card-type. _/ build:_ No debt routing logic in schema. No 'revenue_dept' card identification. crm_deals/customers have no debt_status column. Tasks (if modeled) are kanban-only (no RBAC). Consequence: Q6 'Qarz undirish vazifasi Daromadlar bo'limiga' cannot be auto-routed or permission-checked.
  - **[P2]** Menejer takeover/vacation not addressed in CRM schema - _vizyon:_ Q33 HR aging: If savdo-menejer (card) vakant/on-leave, their leads/deals reassign automatically to next card (by org-chart priority or round-robin). When card transitions from 'Faol' to 'Vakansiya', CRM should trigger: (1) update crm_leads/deals.assigned_card_id → new_manager_card_id, (2) notify old+new managers, (3) update call-routing (corporate-phone → new manager). Requires: (A) card-lifecycle event listener in CRM, (B) lead/deal reassignment logic (preserve history: assigned_history table). _/ build:_ crm_leads, crm_deals use assignedById (user, static). No card-status listener. No reassignment trigger. No assigned_history audit. When a user is marked vakant in HR, CRM leads stay with old user; interaction history lost; corporate-phone call forwarding manual.
  - **[P2]** 360-degree customer view missing card-context - _vizyon:_ Customer 360 should show: (1) primary assigned card (savdo-menejer KARTA), (2) secondary interactions (sifat bo'limi KARTA, Daromadlar KARTA), (3) contact history by card (interaction.card_id + timestamp + type), (4) card-permissions (which team member can see this customer based on card role). Requires: (A) card_id FK on crm_contacts, (B) crm_contact_interactions.card_id (not user_id), (C) permissions checked against card.vysotkiy_function. _/ build:_ customerInteractions.managerId only (not card). crm_contacts.assigned_by_id → user (not card). No card-context in 360 view. Sifat bo'limi interactions (Q4) not modeled separately; would need role-based view ('quality_interactions', but table structure is generic). Result: Q4 vision (Sifat boshlig'i gaplashuvi ham kartada ko'rinadi) cannot be guaranteed; UI shows managerId interactions only.
  - **[P1]** Voronka (pipeline) structure does NOT enforce Vysotskiy-7 org-chart stage mapping - _vizyon:_ CRM voronkas (funnels/pipelines) should mirror org-structure stages: Namuna (lead capture) → Klishe/STP approval (design card approval) → Narx (pricing by savdo card) → Shartnoma (contract, legal card) → Buyurtma (SD order creation) → Final yetkazish (logistics card). Each stage gates on responsible card's approval/signature. Schema: (A) crm_pipeline_stages.required_card_id/required_role (enum: SAVDO, DESIGN, LEGAL, LOGISTICS), (B) lead.current_stage_card_id (card that must approve before transition), (C) state machine: can only transition if current_user.card_id == required_card_id. _/ build:_ crmStages table has statusId, semantics (process/success/fail), but NO org-chart stage mapping. No 'required_card_id' or 'required_role' column. Transitions are open (any user can stage-change crm_leads statusId). Consequence: CRM has no gating by card role; Klishe approval (design card) is manual/external, not enforced; STP pricing (savdo card) is not gated; voronka does NOT mirror Vysotskiy org-structure.
  - **[P1]** No lead-to-SD-order golden-thread mapping - _vizyon:_ Q29-Q30 vision: CRM lead → convert to deal (Q13 ГП code selection) → auto-create SD sales_order with same spec. Lead.status 'CONVERTED' + Deal.created should trigger: (1) sales_order INSERT with deal.title, contact info, product ГП-code, (2) link crm_deals.sales_order_id ↔ sales_orders.id (1:1 for idempotency), (3) notify assigned card's manager. If deal-stage transitions (Ожд.Материал), sales_order auto-updates. Requires: (A) event listener CrmDealCreated → SalesOrderService.create(), (B) crm_deals.sales_order_id FK, (C) sync on deal-stage change. _/ build:_ crmDeals.salesOrderId varchar (FK-like, but not PK of sales_orders; no constraint). Schema suggests intent but no listener/handler. crm-deals.controller.ts likely doesn't trigger SD order creation (E-R check: no event emission visible). Consequence: deal created in CRM, menejer manually creates SD order; dual entry, data divergence, Q29-Q30 'avtomatik ol rejasiga' not realized.

Each deal → papka (unique zavod-internal code); papka contains: zayvka (order form), material spec (gramm, format), cutting-plan. CRM deal.papka_id → manufacturing database. Lead conversion should auto-allocate papka-number (sequential, zavod-wide). Papka-aging (Q10 'o'tgan kun') displayed in CRM deal-card. _/ build:_ No papka table in crm-* schema. No deal.papka_id FK. No manufacturing-spec linkage. PP module (pp-schema.ts) may have this, but CRM ↔ PP linkage not visible in CRM schema (would need cross-module integration). Consequence: Q9-Q12 papka workflow manual; CRM deal title ≠ papka-name; menejer manually looks up papka, risks data sync.

Example: 'Lead #123 status changed NEW→JUNK by card=SAVDO-Menejer-01 (user=Azizov-A) at 2026-06-25 14:30. Reason=No-budget. Approver=Savdo-Boshlig'i card'. Audit trail used for: compliance (tariff hujjati), churn-prevention (lead-junk reasons), performance (card KPI = leads-converted / leads-junk ratio). _/ build:_ crm_leads, crm_deals have dateCreate, dateModify, modifyById (user); no card_id in audit columns. No 'reason' field for stage changes (Q10 kechikish sababi model, but not in CRM). AuditInterceptor logs routes (global), not entity-level mutations. Consequence: Audit trail is user-based (not card-aware); cannot trace 'which bo'lim made decision'; compliance reports (card KPI) incomplete.

### To'liqlik - `PARTIAL` 35%
- **Vizyon kutadi:** VISION (EP-CRM-002, EP-CRM-007, EP-CRM-057, EP-CRM-063 + 8 principle questions): 
1. **Voronka (Sales Funnel)** = 5-step linear pipeline: Namuna (Specimen/Prospect) → Klishe/STP tasdiq (Stamp/Specification approval) → Narx (Pricing) → Shartnoma (Contract) → Buyurtma (Order to SD module). This is the zavod-specific sales process.
2. **Channels** = ALL 4+ (Telegram + WhatsApp + SMS + Email) PLUS menejer FIELD/OUTBOUND visits (tracked; SD-076 source). Auto lead ingest from these channels into CRM lead voronka.
3. **Lead Scoring** = Automated, owner-defined criteria (EP-CRM-012).
4. **Lead Lifecycle Management** = ~60 days inactivity → auto-reassignment to manager pool; loss-reason taxonomy (format/price/deadline/competition); Bitrix-style 360° customer view.
5. **Pricing** = Paper price auto-indexing; ~5% increase → auto alert &amp; "review price" task to affected leads/deals (EP-CRM-057).
6. **Integration** = Bitrix→CSV import (legacy bridge); web lead→CRM (WEB-1); golden thread SD→lead conversion.
7. **Data Model** = Bitrix24-style CRM: leads (Bitrix `b_crm_lead`), deals (from lead conversion), contacts, companies, pipeline stages, activities. Kanonik = `crm_leads` + `crm_deals` (no parallel shadow tables).

**Specific DB expectations**: (A) crm_pipelines + crm_stages populated &amp; linked (voronka structure); (B) crm_proposals with `stp_approved` + `pricing` fields for flow step 2-3; (C) crm_deals.sales_order_id link to golden thread; (D) crm_leads.source_id + auto-channel ingest service; (E) lead scoring algorithm + churn detection (60-day timeout).

- **Qurilgan:** **BUILT (jonli kod + DB):**
- ✅ **Core DB structure**: 27 CRM tables exist (leads, deals, contacts, companies, pipelines, stages, activities, proposals, products, comments, history, robots, watchers, invoices, documents, etc.). Drizzle schema defined across `crm-contacts.ts`, `crm-pipelines.ts`, `crm-deals.ts`, `crm-extended.ts`, `crm-activities.ts`, `crm-proposals.ts`, `crm-deal-products.ts`, `crm-docs.ts`.
- ✅ **Lead + Deal tables**: crm_leads (13 rows seed), crm_deals (4 rows). Columns include sourceId, statusId, phones/emails (JSONB multifield), budget, opportunityAmount, sourceScore (1-100), stage management.
- ✅ **Pipeline stages**: crm_lead_stages table (6 rows: NEW → IN_PROCESS → ANALYSIS → FINAL → CONVERTED/LOST). Semantic semantics (process/success/fail).
- ✅ **Services/Controllers**: 20+ CRM service files (leads-ops, activities, ai, comms, companies, contacts, custom-fields, etc.); 15+ controllers (crm-leads.controller, crm-deals.controller, crm-comms.controller, crm-ai.controller, crm-analytics.controller, etc.). Result pattern adopted (AppErr/Ok/Err).
- ✅ **FE Routes**: /crm-workspace, /crm/funnel, CRMActivities, CRMSettings pages implemented (React components).
- ⚠️ **Sources (Channels)**: seed data shows 5 source_id values (COLD_CALL, REFERRAL, SOCIAL_MEDIA, EXHIBITION, WEBSITE). Only 8 leads have source_id set (out of 13). No Telegram/WhatsApp/SMS/Email explicit channel ingest service observable in code; comms repo hints at it but unclear if real.
- 🔴 **CRITICAL GAPS**:
  1. **Pipelines/Voronka empty**: crm_pipelines table has 0 rows. crm_stages table has 0 rows. VOICE: "voronka" stage flow is undefined in DB. Only crm_lead_stages (hardcoded 6 stages) used; no category-specific pipeline customization.
  2. **Proposals MISSING**: crm_proposals table has 0 rows; no STP (specification) approval step #2 or pricing step #3 observable. No price review/approval workflow.
  3. **No channel auto-ingest**: No observable Telegram/WhatsApp/SMS/Email bot services feeding leads into CRM. comms_controller hints but endpoints return echo/stub (e.g., `POST /crm/comms/send-sms` not implemented).
  4. **Lead-to-Deal workflow vague**: Conversion logic exists (crm-leads-ops.service.convert) but no proposal/pricing gate before deal creation. Direct lead→deal jump, not voronka 5-step flow.
  5. **No golden-thread link**: crm_deals.sales_order_id column exists but unclear if populated on deal→order conversion. Sales_order_id is FK to sales_orders (character varying), but mapping/trigger missing.
  6. **Lead scoring**: crm_leads.sourceScore (1-100) exists; lead-scorer service files exist (4 variants: lead-scorer.service, lead-scorer-v2.service, elo-rating.service), but no active impl' observable. Scoring inputs (criteria) not in DB; no auto-calculate on ingest.
  7. **60-day churn logic**: No crm table for tracking last-activity or auto-reassignment. churn.service exists but read-only analytics (not reactive workflow).
  8. **Bitrix import**: legacy compat layer (crm-bitrix-compat.service) present but no observable seed → no actual Bitrix→ERP link tested.

**Summary**: **Skeleton structure (27 tables, 20 services) built but core VORONKA FLOW GATED**. Pipelines/proposals/pricing/channels unlinked. Leads &amp; deals exist as standalone CRUD, not a coherent sales funnel. **Workflow = echo/stub; visionary 5-step funnel NOT executed.**

  - **[P0]** Voronka (sales funnel) stages EMPTY. Vision requires 5-step: Specimen → STP/Specification → Pricing → Contract → Order. Drag-drop leads between stages on FE (CRM Funnel Analytics). _/ build:_ crm_lead_stages hardcoded (NEW/IN_PROCESS/ANALYSIS/FINAL/CONVERTED/LOST). crm_pipelines + crm_stages tables exist but 0 seed data. No stage customization per pipeline. No pipeline selection on lead creation.
  - **[P0]** Proposal/STP approval (step 2 of voronka) MISSING. Vision: lead → klishe/STP spec approval → pricing → order. ## 17. Xavfsizlik / multi-tenancy - o'rtacha 53% (4 tahlil)

### Asosiy g'oya / Model - `PARTIAL` 35%
- **Vizyon kutadi:** EGASI VIZYONI (master-reja MASSIVE-100 FASE 2, FASE 4, Xavfsizlik standartlari, org-karta spec):

1. **KARTA-MARKAZIY PRINSIP (EP-ORG-003, Q-02, Q-03):** "Kartasiz → login YO'Q + oylik YO'Q." Hozir:
   - card_id NULL bo'lsa login taqiq (gatewayga).
   - card_id NULL bo'lsa oylik-hisobdan skip.
   - Login/RBAC kartadan, eski positions jadvaldan EMAS.

2. **MULTI-TENANCY MODEL (ADR-006):**
   - Column-based: `tenant_id` ustun har jadvalga.
   - AsyncLocalStorage per-request scope (getTenantId()).
   - P0=HR faqat, P2-P3 COMPLETE.

3. **4 GLOBAL GUARD (XAVFSIZLIK STANDARTLARI):**
   - JwtAuthGuard, RolesGuard, PermissionGuard, SodGuard.
   - Vizyon: KARTA-based RBAC (hozir POSITIONS eski).
- **Qurilgan:** QURILGAN:

1. **4 GUARD MAVJUD + ISHLAYDI:** JwtAuthGuard (httpOnly cookie, jti blacklist), RolesGuard (case-insensitive), PermissionGuard (position_permissions), SodGuard (6 qoida).

2. **JWT:** HS256, access/refresh TTL, JTI blacklist.

Pasayish = HR+rahbar+majburiy-sabab. 3-oy guard (oxirgi o'zgarishdan ≥3oy). Oylik-koeffitsiyent (baza×koeff=oylik, FAZA-4 ulaydi). Imtihon o'tish-chegarasi SOZLANADIGAN (qotirilgan emas, egasi beradi). Sertifikat avtomatik + tarix. ЦКП/darslik-gate (FAZA-5/7 ULANADI, bu fazada faqat struktura). **KARTA-MARKAZLI:** razryad hamma kartada (dublikat lavozim=alohida karta). Razryad-qiymatlar egasi-data (fabrikatsiya TAQIQ).

O'tish chegarasi (exam_pass_threshold) = SOZLANADIGAN per-razryad, egasi/HR har razryadga sozlaydi, master-data (EP-ORG-055). 4. Qayta topshirish qoidasi (maxRetakes) = SOZLANADIGAN, egasi sozlaydi (EP-ORG-056). 5. Imtihon turi = A (nazariy+amaliy sinov) (EP-ORG-046). 6. Sertifikat/litsenziya = kartada ro'yxat + amal muddati; 30 kun ogohlantirish (EP-ORG-047). 7. Razryad o'sish = imtihon → HR tasdiq → bevosita rahbar 2-imzo tasdiq → razryad o'zgaradi + ichki sertifikat (EP-ORG-010..013). 8. 3-oy oraliq guard (minMonths) egasi belgilaydi, NULL = RAD (fabrikatsiya TAQIQ) (EP-ORG-011). 9. Razryad pasayish = aniq sababdan (stat-pasayish + takror-xato + qayta-imtihon yiqilish; AI taklif → RD-4 tasdiq) (EP-ORG-134). 10. AI belgilaydi → inson tasdiqlaydi (razryad pasayish FAQAT tasdiq orqali, avtomatik emas; global adolat) (Q128 jarima). 11. Razryad karta ICHIDA ko'rinadi, darajasiga qarab RANGLI (EP-ORG-043). 2. **Tushum 4-hisobga taqsim (EP-FIN-005)**: Yetkazish → (A) AR (Accounts Receivable, 4000) debit ← totalAmount; (B) Revenue (9010) credit ← totalAmount-tax (net VAT); (C) Tax/QQS Payable (6310) credit ← tax (12% UZS); (D) COGS (9100) debit ← costOfGoods; (E) Inventory (1000) credit ← costOfGoods. Barcha 5 leg atomar bitta reference (DC-{orderId}) bilan; refire idempotent.

3. **EP_COST_RATIO (configurated, Q-40 fabrikatsiya TAQIQ)**: `settings.EP_COST_RATIO` table'dan (0<r<1, e.g. 0.65) — hardcode EMAS. Yo'q bo'lsa: (1) revenue legs (AR/Revenue/Tax) HAMMASI post-qladi (receivables/revenue lost bo'lmaydi), lekin (2) COGS legs SKIP-qladi + Telegram-ogohlantirish ("egasi qiymati kerak"). AI inventorta ratio EMAS, Q-40.

4. **Kassa/Kassir = naqd-nazorat markazi (KAS-1, Finance sub-modul)**:
   - **Smena**: openShift (naqd olish) / closeShift (X/Z hisobot, variance=closed-expected);
   - **Harakat**: cash_in/cash_out/salary_payout/advance/expense;
   - **PIN (owner #8)**: cash_out/salary/advance/expense REQUIRE bcrypt-verified 4-raqam PIN (PIN store bo'lmasa → movement gated, forge yo'q);
   - **GL**: har harakat → balanced journal via GlPostingService → canonical `gl_entries` (KAS-{reference}); idempotent;
   - **Harakat turlari (POS-D1 vision)**: EXTERNAL_IN (5-bosqich), EXTERNAL_OUT, INTERNAL, TRANSFER; +/- saldo guard; barcode (EAN-13+Code-128); operator tablet.

5. **Karta↔oylik↔GL zanjir (Faza 4 "oylik kartadan")**: Xodim multi-karta → ulush-yig'indi (`stake_fraction`) → oylik hisob-qilish (`razryad_level_id → coefficient × baza`) → GSD gate (ЦКП deadline o'tmasa) → payroll GL (Dr Salary Expense / Cr Salary Payable, PR-{id}) → cashier payroll-payout (Dr Salary Payable / Cr Cash via KAS-payout).

6. **ATOMARLIK (integrity)**: 
   - Bitta kanonik `gl_entries` jadval (NO `gl_journal_entries`/`gl_lines` dublikat, SAP#76 taqiq);
   - Yagona DDL migration (schema convergent, Faza 0 o'chiradi `org_functions`/`departments` parazitin);
   - Har posting: debit-validation (ΣDr=ΣCr, 0.01 tolerans), double-entry;
   - Reference idempotency (findEntryIdByReference); refire = same entry id return.

7. **INTEGRATION POINTS** (Events):

   - T-14 (DeliveryCompleted): inventory → GL revenue/COGS post;

  - **[P1]** FORM SAVE MAJBURIY: Vision (Qoida 43) = har forma REAL saqlaydi (FE mutation → BE → DB → verification). CashierHub forms — approve/reject button REAL mutatsiyalar qiladi (✅). FinanceDashboard payroll period form — yaratish/hisoblash/yopish real mutations (✅). Lekin: (a) Payroll sheet (xodim oyligi kiritish) UMUMAN FORM YO'Q, (b) Aging/GL config FORM YO'Q. **PARTIAL VALID** (mavjud formalar real saqlaydi). - _vizyon:_ Barcha forme REAL (lekin hozir qaysida forme tuzatilgan bo'lsa ular real). _/ build:_ Mavjud formalar (period/approve) real mutation, lekin **missing forms = missing features**.

### To'g'rilik (ishlaydi != to'g'ri) - `MATCH` 92%
- **Vizyon kutadi:** Yagona kanonik GL daftar (entries), hamma moliya-yozadigan modullar shunga yozadi (POS/Payroll/ZNO/SD). Sales invoice → 5-leg balanced (AR Dr, Revenue Cr, VAT Cr, COGS Dr, Inventory Cr), atomik transaction, idempotent by reference. Har yozuv ikki tomonlama, balanslashtirilmagan yozuv qabul qilinmaydi. Oltin-ip: SD→PP→MES→QC→WMS→FIN real INSERT + DB-proof. Kassir: smena/X-Z hisobot, oylik/avans PIN-gated, kassa limiti. Tannarx versiyali. Xarajat-markazi bo'lim-bo'yicha.
- **Qurilgan:** entries jadval (kanonik, 6 row balanslangan); GL posting service (postDeliveryCompleted 5-leg, postPayroll, postJournal, idempotency by reference); Kassir Hub service (openShift/closeShift PIN-gated); 42 accounts BHMS seed; Payroll/POS/Delivery event-driven GL posting; Zod validatsiya; Drizzle ORM; Result pattern.
- **Chetlashishlar:**
  - **[P1]** Schema drift: debit_account_id/credit_account_id Drizzle varchar, live DB INTEGER FK - _vizyon:_ Schema = live DB (INTEGER FK) _/ build:_ Drizzle varchar, code casts, INSERT ok
  - **[P2]** Kassir FE UI page yo'q (controller bor) - _vizyon:_ Kassir shift management page responsive web _/ build:_ Controller cashier-hub.controller bor, dashboard yo'q
  - **[P2]** Cost-center GL entries'ga biriktirilmagan - _vizyon:_ GL posting cost_center_id qo'shadi, xarajat bo'lim-bo'yicha _/ build:_ Cost center table bor lekin entries posting'da ishlatilmagan
- **Isbot:** `lib/db/src/schema/fi-gl.ts:51-85 — entries kanonik` - `modules/finance/domain/services/gl-posting.service.ts:112-136 — postDeliveryCompleted 5-leg` - `modules/finance/infrastructure/repositories/drizzle-gl-posting.repo.ts:75-122 — insertJournal atomik` - `modules/finance/cashier-hub/cashier-hub.service.ts:71-100 — PIN validation`

# 2-QISM: MASTER REJA - FAZALAR

> Jami chetlashish: **P0=152 / P1=383 / P2=300**. Strategiya: har modulni alohida "toldirish" emas - AVVAL bitta vertikal ip (1 karta -> login -> oylik -> CKP -> 1 buyurtma SD->PP->MES->QC->WMS->FIN->GL) uchma-uch JONLI ishlatish, keyin shablonni kengaytirish.

## FAZA 0 - EGASI-DATA (muhandislik kuta olmaydi)
Bularsiz hech bir karta-yadro ishlamaydi (fabrikatsiya TAQIQ - Q-40):
1. **Bitta daraxt:** 19 root -> 1 Egasi-ildiz + 7 otdeleniye (kim-kimni-boshqaradi). `head_user_id` 126 NULL toldirilsin.
2. **Razryad qiymatlari:** har razryad - salary band (dan-gacha), `exam_pass_threshold`, `min_months` (>=3).
3. **CKP:** norma + deadline (16 soat / 3 soat ZIDDIYATINI hal qil), kurs<->karta biriktirish.
4. **RBAC tier** (har karta) + **AI-kalit** (OpenAI/Gemini).

## FAZA 1 - KARTA-MARKAZLI YADRO (vizyonning "miyasi")
`users.card_id` ustuni; payroll -> karta-salary + razryad-koeff; RBAC position_id->card; 1-karta=1-seat unique guard; login-gate yoqishga tayyorlash. Eng birinchi - qolgani shunga osiladi.

## QABUL MEZONI (har faza)
- Har ozgarish: tsc GREEN (oz fayllarda) + rollback-tx JONLI DB-proof (kirit->oqdi->korindi) + commit.
- Faza tugadi deyiladi FAQAT: vizyon-talab jonli korsatilganda (Q-40 - "ishlaydi" != "togri").
- Egasi-DATA yoq joyda: STRUKTURA + gate quriladi, SOXTA qiymat YOZILMAYDI, egasi-DATA royxatiga qoshiladi.
